### [1] [CTA-Pipelining: A Latency-Oriented Spatial Scaling Method for Multi-GPU Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07862)
*Tingkai Liu,Muralidhar Andoorveedu,Sanjoy Das,Sanjay Patel,Volodymyr Kindratenko*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出CTA-pipelining执行范式，利用共享内存多GPU系统的CTA级依赖实现跨GPU内核并发执行，相比micro-batching和张量并行分别降低31.8%和29.6%的延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算基础设施演进使多GPU系统成为紧密集成的共享内存结构，但现有软件仍将其视为高速网络；同时大语言模型服务需求使GPU负载优化从吞吐驱动转向延迟受限，需要超越张量并行的延迟导向扩展方法。

Method: 提出CTA-pipelining执行范式，作为延迟导向的空间扩展技术，利用Cooperative Thread Array级别的依赖关系，实现跨GPU的依赖内核并发执行。使用CUTLASS、cuBLAS和NCCL库在8-GPU H200和B200系统上演示该能力。

Result: 在代表MLP操作的2层GEMM上，CTA-pipelining相比micro-baching降低延迟高达31.8%，相比张量并行降低29.6%；可作为正交扩展维度与张量并行结合进一步推进延迟边界。

Conclusion: CTA-pipelining成功利用共享内存多GPU系统特性实现延迟优化，为延迟受限的大语言模型服务提供了超越传统张量并行的有效扩展方法，且可与现有技术正交组合。

Abstract: The evolution of compute infrastructure has transformed multi-GPU systems into tightly integrated shared-memory structures. However, current software still mostly treats these coherent interconnects simply as high-speed networks. Simultaneously, the demand for serving Large Language Models under latency constraints has shifted GPU workload optimization from being throughput-driven to latency-bound, necessitating latency-oriented scaling methods beyond Tensor Parallelism (TP).
  Thus, we introduce CTA-pipelining, an execution paradigm designed to exploit shared-memory multi-GPU systems. As a latency-oriented spatial scaling technique, CTA-pipelining leverages dependencies at the Cooperative Thread Array level, enabling concurrent execution of dependent kernels across GPUs. We demonstrate its capability using CUTLASS, cuBLAS, and NCCL libraries on 8-GPU H200 and B200 systems. Results show on 2-layer GEMM, representing the MLP operation, CTA-pipelining reduces latency by up to 31.8% compared to micro-batching, and 29.6% compared to TP. It can also be combined with TP as an orthogonal scaling dimension to further push the latency boundary.

</details>


### [2] [Toward a Unified GPU-Aware OpenSHMEM Specification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08006)
*Naveen Ravi,Nathan Wichmann,Md. Wasi-ur- Rahman,Aurelien Bouteiller,Yıltan Hassan Temuçin,Avinash Kethineedi,Johnathan Alsop,Brandon Potter,Shubhendra Pal Singhal,Jun Shirako,Akihiro Hayashi,Vivek Sarkar,Lawrence C. Stewart,Michael Beebe,Benjamin Michalowicz,Jeongnim Kim,Thiago Teixeria,Mark F. Brown,Aaron Welch,Oscar Hernandez,Wendy Poole,Steve Poole*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了OpenSHMEM GPU感知通信辅助规范，通过GPU作用域内存空间抽象和明确语义，解决了GPU加速器上OpenSHMEM移植性差的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代高性能计算系统以加速器为中心，GPU提供主要的计算和访存能力。然而，现有OpenSHMEM 1.x规范缺乏针对加速器架构的可移植内存模型语义，导致不同GPU厂商的实现在内存管理、能力发现和操作语义上存在差异，严重限制了代码的可移植性和生态系统统一性，这与OpenSHMEM最初统一社区的目标相悖。

Method: 提出OpenSHMEM GPU感知通信辅助规范，作为OpenSHMEM 1.x的轻量级、向后兼容扩展。该规范引入GPU作用域内存空间抽象作为最小内存模型扩展，提供能力查询机制，并明确定义了在RMA、原子操作、同步和集合操作中使用GPU缓冲区的语义。设计以主机发起接口为初始视角，同时提供支持可选设备发起操作的通用语义。

Result: 成功设计了跨多厂商GPU可指定和可实现的GPU感知OpenSHMEM语义。该辅助规范提供了实用的、可快速实现的技术方案，能够在厂商中立规范框架下实现统一，同时为未来OpenSHMEM规范设计提供指导。

Conclusion: GPU感知OpenSHMEM语义可以跨多厂商GPU成功规范化和实现，为在厂商中立规范下统一GPU加速HPC编程提供了切实可行且快速可实现的路径，为未来标准发展奠定基础。

Abstract: Leadership-class HPC systems are now accelerator-centric, with GPUs providing most floating-point throughput and memory bandwidth. As next-generation systems increasingly integrate accelerators through high-speed memory fabrics and system interconnects, exposing larger tightly coupled device domains, \ac{PGAS} models such as OpenSHMEM provide a natural abstraction for expressing fine-grained remote memory operations across these devices. While OpenSHMEM 1.x offers a lean PGAS model for irregular communication, atomics, fine-grained synchronization, and collectives, its memory model lacks portable semantics for accelerator architectures. As a result, existing GPU-enabled OpenSHMEM implementations differ in memory management, capability discovery, and operation semantics, limiting portability and ecosystem cohesion. This risks fracturing the community that OpenSHMEM was originally created to unify.
  This paper proposes an OpenSHMEM Auxiliary Specification for GPU-Aware Communication, designed as a lightweight, backward-compatible extension to OpenSHMEM 1.x. The auxiliary specification introduces a minimal memory model extension via a GPU-scoped memory space abstraction, along with capability queries and well-defined semantics for using \acs{GPU}-attached buffers in RMA, atomic, synchronization, and collective operations. This is initially conceived through the lens of a host-initiated interface, although it provides a general set of semantics that also allow for optional device-initiated support. A central goal of this effort is to demonstrate that GPU-aware OpenSHMEM semantics can be specified and implemented across GPUs from multiple vendors, providing a practical and rapidly implementable step toward unification under a vendor-neutral specification while informing the design of future OpenSHMEM specifications.

</details>


### [3] [On the Limitations of Non-GPU AI Accelerators for Large-Model Inference: A Field Study of MoE and Multimodal Serving on Huawei Ascend](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08215)
*Zheng Yu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于非GPU AI加速器（华为昇腾910）部署大模型推理工作负载的实证研究，详细记录了从CUDA生态迁移过程中遇到的实际工程挑战，总结了8类平台局限性，为评估和运营非GPU加速器的团队提供了可复现的参考。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非GPU AI加速器作为通用GPU的替代方案，在大模型推理中被广泛采用。然而，将高负载工作从CUDA生态迁移到新平台的实际工程成本和挑战缺乏充分文档记录，研究人员需要了解真实的迁移难度和平台成熟度。

Method: 在16卡华为昇腾910系统上，使用CANN和vLLM-Ascend部署两个大规模推理工作负载：（1）基于W8A8 MoE判断模型DeepSeek-V4-Flash的LLM-as-a-judge安全与对齐评估流水线；（2）基于DeepSeek-V4-Flash-Vision的多模态医疗视觉-语言基准测试（MMMU和MMMU-Pro）。通过实际部署过程系统性地识别和记录平台问题。

Result: 为确保工作负载可靠运行，需要对厂商推理插件进行12处源码级补丁、禁用多个高吞吐特性以保持数值正确性、并为反复出现的设备级故障添加操作保护措施。研究将平台局限性归纳为8类：算子和特性支持不完整、并行性脆弱、底层内核数值故障、图编译不成熟、高级特性不稳定、可扩展性受限、可观测性弱、生态系统碎片化。对每类问题报告了症状、证据和可能原因，并量化了集成工作量、并发行为和基准测试质量。

Conclusion: 研究成功在昇腾平台上正确部署了两个大模型推理工作负载，系统性地揭示了非GPU加速器在工程实践中的真实挑战，为评估和运营非GPU加速器的团队提供了可复现的参考经验和问题分类框架。

Abstract: Non-GPU AI accelerators are increasingly adopted as alternatives to general-purpose GPUs for large-model inference, but the real engineering cost of migrating demanding workloads beyond CUDA remains poorly documented. We present a field study of deploying two large inference workloads on a 16-device Huawei Ascend 910 system using CANN and vLLM-Ascend: an LLM-as-a-judge safety and alignment evaluation pipeline based on a W8A8 MoE judge model, DeepSeek-V4-Flash, and a multimodal medical vision--language benchmark based on DeepSeek-V4-Flash-Vision for MMMU and MMMU-Pro. Making these workloads reliable required twelve source-level patches to the vendor inference plugin, disabling several high-throughput features to preserve numerical correctness, and adding operational safeguards for recurring device-level failures. We summarize the main platform limitations in eight categories: incomplete operator and feature support, fragile parallelism, numerical faults in low-level kernels, immature graph compilation, unstable advanced features, limited scalability, weak observability, and ecosystem fragmentation. For each category, we report the symptoms, evidence, and likely causes. We also quantify the integration effort, concurrency behavior, and benchmark quality to show that both workloads were served correctly. Our study provides a reproducible reference for teams evaluating or operating non-GPU accelerators for large-model inference.

</details>


### [4] [Self-Stabilizing Algorithms in the Uniform Port Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08244)
*Liam Brinker,Yuval Emek,Oren Louidor*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了“一致端口”分布式计算模型，并在该模型下首次设计了针对最大独立集、最大匹配、无汇方向等局部对称破缺问题的高效自稳定算法，填补了真正一致性模型下高效算法的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有真正一致的分布式计算模型大多以节点为中心，无法自然表达如最大匹配和无汇方向等半边标号问题，且此前尚无在此类模型下高效自稳定算法的研究。为此，作者引入以端口为中心的一致端口模型，以便统一描述半边标号问题并探索高效自稳定算法的存在性。

Method: 定义一致端口模型：每个图的每个端口（半边）都承载同一个常量大小的有限自动机，算法以局部端口自动机的方式运行。该模型显式支持为半边分配输入和输出标签，适合表达半边标号问题。在此模型下，设计适用于一般图的自稳定算法，通过局部端口间的交互和状态转移实现对最大独立集、最大匹配、无汇方向及节点/边k-染色等问题的求解。

Result: 在一致端口模型下，针对最大独立集、最大匹配、无汇方向及节点/边k-染色等基本局部对称破缺问题，提出了运行时间为多项式对数的自稳定算法。这是首次在真正一致性模型中证明存在高效自稳定算法，且算法适用于一般图。

Conclusion: 一致端口模型不仅扩展了真正一致性模型的表达能力，可自然描述半边标号问题，还首次在该类模型中实现了高效自稳定算法，为局部对称破缺问题在极度受限的一致性模型下的可解性与效率提供了新的理论和算法基础。

Abstract: We introduce a distributed computational model referred to as the \emph{uniform port} model. An algorithm operating in this model is defined by means of local automata associated with the ports (a.k.a.\ half-edges) of the input graph. The crux of the uniform port model is that a single constant-size finite automaton is hosted by every port of every graph, making the model \emph{truly uniform}. Moreover, since the new model explicitly supports the assignment of (input and) output labels to the graph's (half-)edges, it facilitates natural formulations of (half-)edge-labeling problems such as maximal matching and sinkless orientation, which are outside the expressivity scope of prior node-centric truly uniform distributed computational models.
  The main technical contribution of this paper is the design of efficient (i.e., with poly-logarithmic runtime) \emph{self-stabilizing} uniform port algorithms, operating on general graphs, for various fundamental local symmetry breaking problems, including maximal independent set, maximal matching, sinkless orientation, and maximal node/edge $k$-coloring. While efficient self-stabilizing algorithms for local symmetry breaking problems have been extensively studied in stronger computational models, our work is the first to demonstrate the existence of such algorithms in a truly uniform model.

</details>


### [5] [Empirical Analysis of GPU Frequency Behavior Under ML Workloads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08307)
*Truong-Thanh Le,Hoang-Loc La,Amir Taherkordi,Frank Eliassen,Phuong Hoai Ha,Peiyuan Guan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 研究发现NVIDIA GPU在执行ML/AI工作负载时存在频率缩放行为，低性能GPU的运行频率受过去80ms内的工作负载历史影响，这一发现挑战了现有ML延迟预测技术中“GPU内核延迟相互独立”的假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最先进的ML延迟预测技术通常假设各个GPU内核的延迟是独立的，通过累加孤立的单内核测量值来估算总执行时间。本文旨在验证这一假设是否成立，探究GPU动态频率缩放行为对内核间依赖性的影响。

Method: 对NVIDIA GPU执行ML/AI工作负载时的频率缩放行为进行实验研究，重点分析低性能GPU上工作负载历史对运行频率的影响，观察时间窗口约为80ms。

Result: 在低性能GPU上，运行频率强烈受到过去80ms内工作负载历史的影响。GPU的动态频率缩放引入了内核间依赖性，证明“内核延迟相互独立”的假设并不总是成立。

Conclusion: GPU动态频率缩放导致的内核间依赖性对延迟预测有重要影响。未来研究方向包括：改进延迟预测模型、GPU内核重排序策略，以及基于NAS的频率/延迟/能耗感知模型设计指导方针。

Abstract: This work presents ongoing research on the frequency scaling behavior of NVIDIA GPUs when executing ML/AI workloads. Our preliminary findings show that, on lower-performance GPUs, the operating frequency is strongly affected by the recent workload history, typically within an 80ms window. This behavior challenges a common assumption underlying several state-of-the-art ML latency-prediction techniques, which treat individual GPU kernel latencies as independent and therefore estimate total execution time by summing isolated per-kernel measurements. Our results indicate that such an assumption does not always hold, as the GPU's dynamic frequency scaling introduces inter-kernel dependencies. We also outline several promising directions for leveraging this observation in future work, including improved latency-prediction models, GPU kernel-reordering strategies, and NAS-driven guidelines for frequency/latency/energy-aware model design.

</details>


### [6] [Adaptive Row Selection Meets Asynchrony in Randomized Kaczmarz](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08313)
*Evan Coleman*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 首次系统研究异步执行环境下自适应Kaczmarz算法的稳定性，发现采样激进程度与线程数存在临界边界，不一致读取比一致快照更安全且高效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机化Kaczmarz算法适用于大规模稀疏最小二乘和层析重建，自适应行选择可减少迭代次数。但在共享内存机器上部署自适应选择时，无锁工作线程并发修改残差会导致使用陈旧数据，该异步执行 regime 的行为尚未被系统研究。

Method: 在96核节点上对339次真实延迟运行进行系统测量，研究残差加权和贪婪Kaczmarz在异步执行下的表现，分析采样激进程度与线程数的边界关系，测试欠松弛策略和不同读取策略的影响。

Result: 发现四个关键结论：(i) 稳定性由采样激进程度与线程数的边界ℓ*(T)决定，边界以下更激进的采样严格更优；(ii) 阈值贪婪选择在高线程数时不稳定且几乎立即发散；(iii) 欠松弛可预测地恢复稳定性边界，提供可用的安全调节；(iv) 一致快照读取存在罕见的调度相关发散，而不一致读取从未出现且成本更低。

Conclusion: 给出了异步自适应Kaczmarz的实践指导原则：应将参数调至稳定性边界内侧；不一致读取应作为默认选择；欠松弛提供可预测的安全性。研究为分布式两层采样器的设计提供了依据。

Abstract: Randomized Kaczmarz is a natural fit for large sparse least-squares and tomographic reconstruction, and adaptive row selection can reduce iteration counts. However, deploying adaptive selection on a shared-memory machine means sampling from a residual that lock-free workers are concurrently modifying, often using stale data. We present the first systematic study of this regime: residual-weighted and greedy Kaczmarz under asynchronous execution, measured across 339 runs on a 96-core node with realized (not injected) delays. Four findings carry directly to practice. (i) Stability is governed by a boundary $\ell^*(T)$ between sampling aggressiveness and thread count; below it, more aggressive sampling is strictly better, so one should tune to just inside the cliff. (ii) Threshold-greedy selection (the standard accelerated rule) is unstable at high thread counts, diverging almost immediately. (iii) Under-relaxation buys back the cliff at a predictable cost, giving a usable safety knob. (iv) Consistent-snapshot reads admit a rare, scheduling-dependent divergence that live (inconsistent) reads never exhibited and that is also cheaper, making inconsistent reads the right default. We validate the implementation against published sequential results and outline the distributed two-level sampler these measurements motivate.

</details>


### [7] [Computing in Anonymous Dynamic Networks with One-Bit Communications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08358)
*Thibaut Blanc,Giuseppe Antonio Di Luna,Giovanni Viglietta*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文首次研究匿名动态网络中的确定性计算，证明了即使每轮仅广播1比特，也能实现与标准拥塞模型相当的丰富全局计算能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在极端通信限制下的计算能力边界——匿名动态网络中每个代理每轮仅能广播1比特，且只能接收邻居广播的比特计数（而非具体内容）。研究这种严重受限的通信模型是否仍能支持有意义的全局计算。

Method: 1）从局部1比特聚合观测中提取全局线性方程；2）利用1比特切割测试生成代理类别大小的守恒约束；3）通过精炼类别和收集独立约束恢复所需的乘数；4）针对未知网络规模，提出一种具有独立研究价值的自纠正自适应泛洪原语。

Result: 上界：存在唯一领导者且已知网络规模上界U时，给出可在O(n³log²n+U)轮内终止的算法；未知n时给出O(n³log²n)轮的稳定算法。这基本匹配了标准拥塞模型O(n³)轮的最新技术水平。下界：证明 rounds下界，当N=2^{Ω(n log n)}时变为Ω(n³)。该下界即使在有领导者、已知n和N、通信图限于动态环的条件下仍成立。

Conclusion: 拥塞匿名动态网络的计算能力在每条消息被压缩至1比特时得以基本保留。研究揭示了1比特通信模型具有令人惊讶的丰富计算能力，为受限通信环境下的分布式计算提供了新的理论基础。

Abstract: We initiate the study of deterministic computation in anonymous dynamic networks where each agent broadcasts one bit per round and receives only the number of neighbors broadcasting each bit value. Despite this severe restriction, surprisingly rich global computation is possible.
  With a unique leader and a known upper bound $U$ on the network size $n$, we give a terminating algorithm for any computable function of the input multiset in $O(n^3\log^2 n+U)$ rounds, for inputs from a universe of size $N=2^{O(n\log n)}$. Without prior knowledge of $n$, we design a stabilizing algorithm for the same task running in $O(n^3\log^2 n)$ rounds. This essentially matches the state of the art for the congested model, where messages carry $O(\log n)$ bits and general computation takes $O(n^3)$ rounds. We also obtain comparable results for leaderless and multi-leader networks.
  We complement the upper bounds with an almost-matching lower bound of $$Ω!\left(\frac{n^2\log(N/n)}{\log n}\right)$$ rounds, which becomes $Ω(n^3)$ for $N=2^{Ω(n\log n)}$. The proof is information-theoretic, based on local histories, and holds even with a unique leader, known $n$ and $N$, and a communication graph restricted to a dynamically changing ring.
  Our algorithms extract global linear equations from local one-bit aggregate observations. A one-bit cut test yields conservation constraints on the sizes of indistinguishable agent classes; by refining these classes and collecting independent constraints, agents recover the required multiplicities. For unknown size, we introduce a self-correcting adaptive flooding primitive of independent interest. Thus, the computational power of congested anonymous dynamic networks is essentially preserved even when every message is compressed to one bit.

</details>


### [8] [Coded Task Offloading for Fluid Computing: A Privacy-Aware Approach under D2D Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08440)
*Diego Cajaraville-Aboy,Manuel Fernández-Veiga,Ana Fernández-Vilas,Rebeca P. Díaz-Redondo*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种编码任务卸载方案，将任务卸载与线性秘密共享相结合，在D2D网络中实现了延迟、能耗和隐私保护的联合优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有任务卸载方案很少解决对抗性执行环境下的隐私风险和信息泄露问题；大多数编码计算方案仅关注消除拖后腿效应（straggler mitigation），而忽略了能耗感知等系统级目标。

Method: 将任务卸载技术与线性秘密共享方案相结合，将任务编码为冗余份额以支持阈值恢复、拖后腿效应缓解和隐私保护；建立隐私感知卸载问题，联合优化延迟和能耗并对编码任务的理论隐私泄露进行惩罚；采用分支定界求解器和轻量级启发式调度器求解该问题。

Result: 编码卸载相比传统的完全卸载和并行卸载方案改善了延迟-能耗权衡；启发式算法实现近最优性能，优于基线和最先进求解器；隐私泄露惩罚重塑了卸载决策，揭示了内在的延迟-能耗-隐私权衡关系。

Conclusion: 所提编码任务卸载方案成功在D2D网络中实现了性能优化与隐私保护的平衡，为动态和隐私敏感环境下的分布式应用执行提供了有效解决方案，展现了延迟、能耗与隐私三者之间的内在权衡关系。

Abstract: Fluid Computing aims to support distributed applications execution across heterogeneous cloud, edge, and device resources, motivating task execution mechanisms that adapt to dynamic and privacy-sensitive environments under runtime conditions. In this context, current task offloading schemes rarely address privacy risks and information leakage under adversarial execution settings; furthermore, most coded computing proposals focus on straggler mitigation without considering system-level objectives such as energy awareness. This paper proposes a coded task offloading scheme for D2D networks under stochastic task arrivals and queue-based dynamics. The proposal combines task offloading techniques with linear secret sharing schemes, where tasks are encoded into redundant shares to support threshold-based recovery, straggler mitigation, and privacy preservation while enhancing system performance. Then, we formulate a privacy-aware offloading problem that jointly optimizes delay and energy while penalizing the theoretical privacy leakage of coded tasks under noisy leakage observations. The problem is solved using a branch-and-bound solver alongside a lightweight heuristic scheduler, both of which are evaluated through a discrete-event simulator. Results show that coded offloading improves the delay--energy trade-off with respect to classical full and parallel offloading schemes, while the heuristic achieves near-optimal performance, outperforming baseline and state-of-the-art solvers. The results also show how privacy leakage penalties reshape offloading decisions, exposing an inherent delay--energy--privacy trade-off.

</details>


### [9] [SMetric: Rethink LLM Scheduling for Serving Agents with Balanced Session-centric Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08565)
*Jiahao Wang,Kaizhan Lin,Kaixi Zhang,Jinbo Han,Xingda Wei,Sijie Shen,Chenguang Fang,Wenyuan Yu,Rong Chen,Haibo Chen*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文针对智能体LLM服务场景，提出SMETRIC调度器，通过会话首请求负载均衡、后续请求缓存感知的策略，在保持高KV缓存复用的同时实现集群负载均衡，提升TPS 10-34%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM调度器针对人类用户设计，而智能体服务场景存在两大变化：(1)智能体仅作用于完整响应，集群TPS成为首要目标；(2)请求间KV缓存复用率显著提高（生产环境达80%+）。现有调度器为提高KV复用率过度优先路由到缓存实例，导致负载不均、TPS受限。

Method: 提出SMETRIC会话中心调度器：利用全局KV存储和会话内局部性，将会话首请求纯按负载均衡路由，后续请求以缓存感知方式路由，仅均衡少量请求即可实现集群均衡并保留大部分本地KV复用。调度指标（会话轮次）仅从用户输入高效准确推导，调度器保持无状态。

Result: 在预填充-解码同置+全局存储场景下集群TPS提升10-16%；在解耦架构下预填充TPS提升2-34%，同时改善每词元延迟。

Conclusion: SMETRIC通过会话中心调度策略，在智能体服务场景成功平衡了负载均衡与KV缓存复用，显著优于现有调度器，验证了会话信息作为调度指标的有效性。

Abstract: LLM scheduling is critical to serving, yet it remains unclear how well existing designs fit agentic serving--with LLM requests issued by agents instead of humans. This shifts the workload in two ways: (1) agents act only on complete responses, making the cluster's tokens per second (TPS) the primary goal and relaxing--not eliminating--per-token latency requirements; and (2) requests share much of their KV\$-reuse exceeds 80% of request tokens in a production trace from BAILIAN, versus 54-62% in chat.
  This paper first contributes a systematic study of request scheduling for agents on two real-world traces. We find that to increase KV\$ reuse, existing schedulers overly prioritize routing requests to instances caching their KV\$, overloading a few while leaving the rest idle, capping TPS. We thus present two key insights: (1) load balance need not sacrifice all KV\$ reuse, thanks to the global-tier KV\$ store and (2) by utilizing the workload's intra-session locality, balancing a small fraction of requests--the first request in each agent session--suffices to balance the cluster without sacrificing most KV\$ reuse on local instances.
  SMETRIC realizes these insights with balanced session-centric scheduling: it routes each session's first request purely for load balance and its follow-up requests in a cache-aware manner, preserving load balance and local reuse while keeping demand on the global tier low. Using the session turn information as the scheduling metric is deliberate: it is derived efficiently and accurately from the user inputs alone, so the scheduler stays clean and stateless. SMETRIC improves cluster TPS by 10-16% under prefill-decode colocation with a global store and prefill TPS by 2-34% under disaggregation over state-of-the-art schedulers, also with a better per-token latency.

</details>

### [10] [Towards the Explainability of Temporal Graph Networks via Memory Backtracking and Topological Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07716)
*Yazheng Liu,Xi Zhang,Sihong Xie,Hui Xiong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MemExplainer方法，通过拓扑归因树和记忆回溯树解释TGN预测，解决了现有方法忽视记忆模块的问题，在9个数据集上超越现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时序图网络(TGNs)在预测准确性方面表现优异，但现有解释方法忽视了记忆模块这一核心组件，未能探索历史事件对模型预测的影响。理解哪些历史事件驱动模型预测对于增强TGNs的可信度至关重要。

Method: 提出MemExplainer框架：(1)构建拓扑归因树捕获邻居及其记忆向量的影响；(2)构建记忆回溯树量化历史事件如何塑造节点记忆向量；(3)在TGNs中应用LRP方法，确保事件总贡献等于模型logits；(4)设计优化目标识别重要事件，解决top-k选择因logits到概率非线性映射导致的不忠实问题。

Result: 在9个时序图数据集上进行实验，涵盖节点属性预测、链接预测和图分类任务。实验结果表明该方法提供了忠实的解释，并优于最先进的基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模记忆模块和历史事件的影响，MemExplainer为TGNs提供了更忠实的解释，填补了现有解释方法的空白，为时序图神经网络的可解释性研究提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Temporal graphs are ubiquitous in real-world applications and Temporal Graph Networks (TGNs) have achieved superior predictive accuracy. Understanding which historical events drive model predictions can enhance trustworthiness of TGNs. Existing explanation methods overlook the memory module, the core component that records and updates node histories, leaving the influence of past events unexplored. To address this, we attribute TGNs predictions through the topology attribution tree and memory backtracking tree. The topology attribution tree captures the influence of neighbors and their memory vectors, then the memory backtracking tree quantifies how historical events shape node memory vectors. We apply the LRP in TGNs, ensuring that the total contribution of events equals the logits of model. Finally, top-k selection may be unfaithful due to the nonlinear mapping from logits to probabilities, we design optimization objectives to identify the important events. Experiments on nine temporal graph datasets, spanning node property prediction, link prediction tasks and graph classification tasks, show that our method provides faithful explanations and outperforms state-of-the-art baselines. The code is available at https://github.com/yazhengliu/MemExplainer

</details>


### [11] [Who Gets Missed in the Tail? Thresholded Subgroup Underdiagnosis in Long-Tailed Chest X-ray Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07717)
*Ha-Hieu Pham,Hai-Dang Nguyen,Dang P. M. Cao,Thanh-Huy Nguyen,Min Xu,Trung-Nghia Le,Ulas Bagci,Huy-Hieu Pham*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对胸部X光分类中的长尾分布问题，研究发现通过群体感知加权和尾部分类阈值调整可显著降低稀有疾病和敏感子群体的漏诊率，揭示了公平性依赖于疾病类型、子群体和操作阈值的共同作用，而非仅依赖标签频率或排序指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在胸部X光（CXR）分类任务中，即使排序性能良好，稀有阳性患者仍可能被遗漏在决策阈值之下，尤其是在特定子群体中。研究者将这种部署前的公平性问题界定为一个审计问题：当长尾多标签CXR模型的预测分数转换为决策后，哪些患者会被漏诊？这揭示了现有模型在罕见疾病和弱势群体中的系统性偏见。

Method: 研究者在VinDr-CXR和MIMIC-CXR/CXR-LT数据集上，使用诊断阶梯方法系统分析了四个关键因素：类别级长尾损失、子群体感知加权、群体鲁棒性和阈值选择。具体采用了群体尾部加权结合尾部感知阈值调整的策略，并通过配对Bootstrap对比和GroupDRO参考实验进行验证。这种方法将评分模型的性能转化为决策性能进行评估。

Result: 在VinDr-CXR上，群体尾部加权配合尾部感知阈值调整将尾部假阴性率（FNR）从0.665降至0.269，性别最差群体FNR从0.705降至0.157，年龄最差群体FNR从0.822降至0.133，同时宏平均精度（macro-mAP）从0.611提升至0.635。在MIMIC-CXR/CXR-LT上，相同方法将尾部FNR从0.866降至0.741，并降低了性别、年龄、种族和保险类型的最差群体FNR，但残余漏诊率仍然较高。GroupDRO实验表明单纯的聚合群体鲁棒性优化无法消除此场景下的稀有子群体遗漏。

Conclusion: 研究得出了一个明确的审计结论：CXR中稀有标签的公平性依赖于疾病发现、子群体和操作阈值的共同作用，而非仅依赖标签频率或排序指标。这为医疗AI系统的部署前审计提供了重要指导，强调了需要针对特定疾病-子群体组合进行阈值优化的必要性。

Abstract: In chest X-ray (CXR) classification, acceptable ranking performance can still leave rare-positive patients below threshold, especially within subgroups. We study this pre-deployment fairness problem as an audit question: after a long-tailed multi-label CXR model is converted from scores into decisions, who is missed? Across VinDr-CXR and MIMIC-CXR/CXR-LT, we use a diagnostic ladder to separate class-level long-tail losses, subgroup-aware weighting, group robustness, and threshold selection. On VinDr-CXR, group-tail weighting followed by tail-aware thresholding reduces tail FNR from 0.665 to 0.269, sex worst-group FNR from 0.705 to 0.157, and age worst-group FNR from 0.822 to 0.133, while macro-mAP increases from 0.611 to 0.635. On MIMIC-CXR/CXR-LT, the same score-to-threshold comparison reduces tail FNR from 0.866 to 0.741 and lowers worst-group FNR across sex, age, race, and insurance; residual missed-positive rates nevertheless remain high. Paired bootstrap contrasts on VinDr support the thresholded FNR reductions, and GroupDRO reference runs indicate that aggregate group robustness alone does not remove rare subgroup misses in this setting. The study supports a narrow audit claim: rare-label fairness in CXR depends jointly on the finding, subgroup, and operating threshold, not on label frequency or ranking metrics alone.

</details>


### [12] [LLT: Local Linear Transformer for PDE Operator Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07718)
*Oded Ovadia,Eli Turkel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了局部线性Transformer（LLT）用于PDE算子学习，通过结合线性全局注意力和局部空间混合机制，在降低计算复杂度的同时提升了性能，在多个PDE问题上取得了具有竞争力或更低的相对L2误差，并将每次训练迭代的时钟时间减少了1.8到2.5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于Transformer的神经算子在处理偏微分方程（PDE）时面临两大局限：标准注意力机制的计算复杂度随计算节点数呈二次方增长，且缺乏对局部相互作用的显式偏置，这限制了其在PDE求解中的效率和准确性。

Method: 引入了局部线性Transformer（LLT）架构，该架构结合了线性全局注意力机制与局部空间混合，并融入了坐标和几何信息，从而在保持捕捉长程依赖能力的同时，降低了计算复杂度并增强了局部交互建模能力。

Result: 在弹性、塑性、翼型流动、管道流动和达西流动等多个PDE问题上进行了评估，覆盖了有限元、有限体积和有限差分离散化方法。与现有基线相比，LLT在所有问题上取得了具有竞争力或更低的相对L2误差；在匹配的结构化离散化上，每次训练迭代的时钟时间相比Transolver减少了1.8到2.5倍；成功扩展应用于包含32,186个非结构化网格点的三维汽车空气动力学数据集。

Conclusion: LLT为跨离散化方法、网格类型和问题设置的PDE问题提供了一个精确且计算高效的算子，证明了其在加速数值模拟和学习PDE解映射方面的有效性和通用性。

Abstract: Neural operators have become a common approach for learning PDE solution maps and accelerating numerical simulations. Transformer-based neural operators are of particular interest, since attention can learn long-range dependencies in the computational domain. However, standard attention has two major limitations when applied to PDEs: it scales quadratically with the number of computational nodes, and it lacks an explicit bias toward local interactions. To address these issues, we introduce Local Linear Transformer (LLT) for PDE operator learning. The architecture combines linear global attention with local spatial mixing, and incorporates coordinate and geometry information. We evaluate LLT on several PDE problems, including elasticity, plasticity, airfoil flow, pipe flow, and Darcy flow. The reference data for these problems span finite-element, finite-volume, and finite-difference discretizations on structured and unstructured meshes. Compared with other neural-operator and transformer baselines from prior studies, LLT achieves competitive or lower relative $L_2$ error across these problems. On matched structured discretizations, wall-clock time per training iteration is reduced by factors of 1.8 to 2.5 relative to Transolver. We also scale the approach and apply it to a three-dimensional car aerodynamics dataset with 32,186 unstructured mesh points per sample. Together, these results indicate that LLT provides an accurate and computationally efficient operator for PDE problems across discretizations, mesh types, and problem settings.

</details>


### [13] [ReCoLoRA: Spectrum-Aware Recursive Consolidation for Continual LLM Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07719)
*Wentao Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ReCoLoRA通过递归分解和整合已学习的低秩更新，解决了LoRA在持续学习中灾难性遗忘的问题，在GLUE基准测试上以更少参数实现了优于现有方法的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LoRA风格的参数高效微调方法在持续学习场景中存在关键缺陷：当按序列处理多个任务时，新任务的低秩更新会覆盖之前任务的更新，导致灾难性遗忘，因为所有任务都在同一个冻结权重上堆叠更新。

Method: ReCoLoRA提出递归整合框架：1）使用预训练权重的随机化SVD初始化适配器，通过肘部法则选择每层的有效秩；2）先适应主子空间，再开启剩余容量；3）核心机制是在每个新任务前重新分解当前有效权重（而非原始权重），将其分解为冻结残差、缓慢更新的主成分和新鲜适配器，使每个任务都从已吸收前序任务知识的模型开始。

Result: 在四个7-8B骨干模型上的六任务GLUE持续学习序列中，ReCoLoRA在三个骨干上取得了最佳的最终平均得分，超越了经过秩扫描优化的LoRA、PiSSA、AdaLoRA和DoRA基线，同时训练参数更少；oracle路由的任务库变体在全任务隔离下作为上界参考。

Conclusion: ReCoLoRA成功实现了参数高效的持续学习，通过递归整合机制有效缓解了灾难性遗忘问题，证明了在持续微调场景中显式整合历史任务知识的必要性和有效性。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning adapts a large language model to one task cheaply, but across a task sequence LoRA-style methods keep stacking low-rank updates on the same frozen weight, so each new task tends to overwrite the previous ones. We present ReCoLoRA (Recursive Consolidation of Low-Rank Adapters), a spectrum-aware framework for continual fine-tuning: adapters are initialized from a randomized SVD of the pretrained weight, per-layer effective ranks are selected by an elbow criterion, and the principal subspace is adapted before residual capacity is opened. Before each new task, ReCoLoRA re-decomposes the current effective weight, rather than the original one, into a frozen residual, a slowly updated principal component, and a fresh adapter (recursive consolidation), so every task starts from the model that has already absorbed its predecessors. On a six-task continual GLUE sequence over four 7-8B backbones, ReCoLoRA attains the best final average score on three of the four backbones against rank-swept LoRA, PiSSA, AdaLoRA, and DoRA baselines while training fewer parameters; an oracle-routed task-bank variant serves as an upper bound under full task isolation. Code: https://github.com/bhqy666/ReCoLoRA.

</details>


### [14] [Omni-Sleep: A Sleep Foundation Model via Hierarchical Contrastive Learning of CNS--ANS Dynamic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07720)
*Zhoujie Hou,Song Wang,Kexin Lou,Mo Wang,Chen Wei,Quanying Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Omni-Sleep，一种基于CNS/ANS生理分层先验的睡眠基础模型，通过拓扑约束表示学习在睡眠分期和多疾病分类任务中显著优于现有基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有睡眠基础模型通常以拓扑无关的方式融合异构生物信号，忽略了多导睡眠图(PSG)信号(EEG、EOG、EMG、ECG、呼吸)的生理组织结构。睡眠生理源于中枢神经系统(CNS)和自主神经系统(ANS)的协调动力学，需要建模这些系统间的生理层级关系。

Method: 提出Omni-Sleep模型，利用CNS/ANS分区作为生理先验进行拓扑约束表示学习。设计三个训练目标：(1)系统内一致性——捕获神经和心肺呼吸信号内的子系统级共享因素；(2)系统间同步性——对齐子系统轨迹以建模脑-体动力学；(3)潜在空间掩码时序建模——捕获长时睡眠动态。在超过10万小时的多中心多模态PSG数据上进行预训练。

Result: 在睡眠分期和多疾病分类任务上，Omni-Sleep在多个数据集和模态消融设置下均优于强基线模型，展现出更好的标签效率、跨数据集泛化能力和对缺失模态的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 研究结果凸显了生理层级信息对可泛化睡眠表示学习的价值，证明了将生理学先验知识融入深度学习模型的重要性。

Abstract: Sleep physiology arises from the coordinated dynamics of the central nervous system (CNS) and autonomic nervous system (ANS), as reflected by multimodal polysomnography signals including EEG, EOG, EMG, ECG, and respiration. However, existing sleep foundation models often fuse heterogeneous biosignals in a topology-agnostic manner, overlooking their physiological organization. We introduce Omni-Sleep, a sleep foundation model that uses the CNS/ANS partition as a physiological prior for topology-constrained representation learning. Omni-Sleep learns structured representations through three objectives: intra-system consistency, which captures shared subsystem-level factors within neural and cardio-respiratory signals; inter-system synchronization, which aligns subsystem trajectories to model brain--body dynamics; and latent-space masked temporal modeling, which captures long-horizon sleep dynamics. Pre-trained on over 100,000 hours of multi-center multimodal PSG data, Omni-Sleep is evaluated on sleep staging and multi-disease classification. Across datasets and modality-ablation settings, Omni-Sleep outperforms strong foundation-model baselines, showing improved label efficiency, cross-dataset generalization, and robustness to missing modalities. These results highlight the value of physiological hierarchy for generalizable sleep representation learning. Code is available at https://github.com/AutoBrain-sleep/OmniSleep.

</details>


### [15] [Uncertainty-gated selection for block-sparse attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07724)
*Thomas Rossi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种信息价值路由器，通过识别top-k选择中的临界情况并动态扩展保留的键块集合，显著提升了块稀疏注意力机制在长上下文任务中的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 块稀疏注意力机制通过每查询top-k选择替代O(N²)softmax来扩展长上下文语言模型，但该截断方式存在短视问题：当第k和第k+1个块的分数几乎相等时，选择器会做出不可逆的决定，丢弃的块可能包含关键答案证据且无法在下游恢复。

Method: 提出信息价值路由器，对每个查询测量top-k截断的确定性程度，并为间隔最小的查询将保留集扩大一倍。该规则与主干网络无关，可与现有的块评分方法（如Quest）叠加使用。

Result: 在LongBench-v2 medium（n=215完整数据集子集）上，路由器配合Quest达到配对召回率0.75，而top-k基线仅0.47，相比SSA风格基线提升28个百分点（McNemar p<0.01）；在RULER NIAH multikey上与密集注意力准确率差距在2个百分点以内；在128K上下文长度下，Qwen2.5-7B-1M和Qwen3.6分别保留密集注意力准确率的0.81和0.89，融合选择加内核流水线的运行时间为密集注意力的0.62x和0.80x。

Conclusion: 信息价值路由器成功解决了块稀疏注意力中的临界截断问题，在保持计算效率的同时显著提升了长上下文任务的准确性，该方法是主干无关的，在三个架构的四个模型上均复现了性能提升。

Abstract: Block-sparse attention scales long-context language models by replacing the O(N^2) softmax with a per-query top-k selection over key blocks. This cutoff is myopic: when the k-th and (k+1)-th blocks are nearly tied in score, the selector commits without spending extra budget, and a dropped block carrying answer evidence is unrecoverable downstream. We propose a value-of-information router that measures, for each query, how decisively the top-k cut was made, and doubles the kept set for the queries where that gap is smallest; the rule is backbone-agnostic and stacks with existing block-scoring methods such as Quest. On LongBench-v2 medium at n=215 (the entire dataset subset), router-on-Quest reaches paired recall 0.75 vs. top-k 0.47 -- +28 pp over the SSA-style baseline (McNemar p<0.01) -- and lands within 2 pp of dense on RULER NIAH multikey at the same context. The lift reproduces on four models from three architectures (Qwen2.5, Mistral-Nemo, Qwen3.6). At 128K, the router preserves 0.81 and 0.89 of dense accuracy on Qwen2.5-7B-1M and Qwen3.6 (vs. SSA-style top-k at 0.09 on the former) while the fused selection-plus-kernel pipeline runs at 0.62x and 0.80x dense wall time.

</details>


### [16] [SHIFT: Survival Prediction from Incomplete and Heterogeneous Genomic Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07725)
*Muhammet Sami Yavuz,Ayhan Can Erdur,Sabri Mustafa Kahya,Benedikt Wiestler,Jana Lipkova*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了SHIFT模型，一种基于Transformer的缺失感知生存预测方法，可直接从不完整基因组输入进行预测，无需测试时插补，在多中心数据上展现出良好的泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基因组预测模型难以在不同机构间迁移，因为各中心的测序面板不同，导致部署时存在结构性特征缺失。现有方法通常限制分析于共享基因、排除不完整特征的患者或依赖测试时插补，这些方法降低了鲁棒性并限制了多中心数据的使用。

Method: SHIFT（Survival prediction Handling Incomplete Features using Transformer）是一种缺失感知生存模型。该方法将每个基因组特征单独表示，使用带特征可用性掩码的掩码自注意力机制，确保预测仅基于观测到的输入。此外，在训练过程中引入变速特征掩码以提高对异质性缺失模式的鲁棒性。

Result: 在胶质母细胞瘤和肺鳞状细胞癌数据上进行评估，并在多个队列中进行外部验证，包括严重的跨队列面板不匹配场景。SHIFT在所有设置中均表现出强大的泛化能力，优于标准生存基线和基于插补的方法，同时可在不同特征集上使用单一模型。研究发现，在模型开发中纳入不完整队列的患者可以提高外部数据性能。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明部分观测的队列不必从模型构建中排除，缺失感知建模是精准肿瘤学中多中心生存预测的实用策略。

Abstract: Genomic prediction models often fail to transfer across institutions because sequencing panels differ across sites, creating structural feature missingness at deployment. Existing approaches to this challenge typically restrict analysis to genes shared across cohorts, exclude patients with incomplete profiles, or rely on test-time imputation, all of which can reduce robustness and limit the use of multi-center data. We propose Survival prediction Handling Incomplete Features using Transformer (SHIFT), a missingness-aware survival model that directly predicts from incomplete genomic inputs without test-time imputation. SHIFT represents each genomic feature separately and uses masked self-attention, along with a feature-availability mask, so that predictions are based only on observed inputs. Further, we introduce variable-rate feature masking during training to improve robustness to heterogeneous missingness patterns. We evaluate the approach on glioblastoma and lung squamous cell carcinoma with external validation across multiple cohorts, including a challenging setting with severe cross-cohort panel mismatch. Across these settings, SHIFT shows strong generalization and compares favorably with standard survival baselines and imputation-based approaches, while using a single model across differing feature sets. We also find that incorporating patients from incomplete cohorts during development can improve performance on external data, suggesting that partially observed cohorts need not be excluded from model building. These results support missingness-aware modeling as a practical strategy for multi-center survival prediction in precision oncology.

</details>


### [17] [Jet-Long: Efficient Long-Context Extension with Dynamic Bifocal RoPE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07740)
*Haozhan Tang,Zerui Wang,Yuxian Gu,Song Han,Han Cai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Jet-Long，一种无需调参的零样本上下文扩展方法，通过动态调整重缩放因子，在短上下文完全恢复基模型性能，在长上下文实现干净外推，并实现近零推理开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代LLM在长上下文应用中常需突破预训练窗口限制，但现有零样本方法因固定单一重缩放因子，面临短上下文保真度与长上下文有效性的权衡困境。

Method: 采用双焦窗口策略：局部RoPE保真窗口 + 动态调整重缩放因子的长距离窗口；通过包含-排除注意力融合和即时RoPE校正旋转实现高效推理，集成到单个CuTe kernel中。

Result: 在Qwen3-1.7B/4B/8B（128K上下文）上，RULER基准分别比最强基线提升+4.79/+2.18/+2.03个百分点；HELMET-RAG达到最佳整体准确率；PG-19困惑度最低；H100上长上下文预填充吞吐量达FA2的1.39倍，单批次生成开销≤4%。

Conclusion: Jet-Long成功解决了零样本上下文扩展中短-长上下文性能权衡问题，兼具高效推理和卓越性能，且对混合注意力架构和超参数具有良好泛化性与鲁棒性，便于实际部署。

Abstract: Modern LLMs are increasingly deployed in long-context applications such as retrieval-augmented generation, repository-level coding, and agentic workflows whose accumulated reasoning and tool traces routinely push the input an order of magnitude past the pretraining window, making zero-shot context extension the dominant deployment path for open-weight checkpoints. Most existing zero-shot methods fix a single rescaling factor up front, so an aggressive factor sacrifices short-context fidelity while a conservative one breaks down at long contexts. We propose Jet-Long, a tuning-free zero-shot method that pairs a local RoPE-faithful window with a long-range window whose rescaling factor adapts dynamically to the current sequence length, recovering the base model exactly at short inputs while extrapolating cleanly at long ones. An inclusion-exclusion attention merge and an on-the-fly RoPE correction rotation make the bifocal construction essentially free at inference; fused into a single CuTe kernel, long-context prefill reaches up to $1.39\times$ FA2 throughput on H100 (approaching the Hopper-only FA4), and single-batch generation incurs $\le 4\%$ overhead at every length. On Qwen3-1.7B/4B/8B up to 128K context, Jet-Long leads RULER by $+4.79$/$+2.18$/$+2.03$~pp over the strongest baseline at 1.7B/4B/8B, achieves the best overall accuracy on HELMET-RAG (a benchmark identified by HELMET as the most efficient predictor of downstream long-context performance) and attains the lowest PG-19 perplexity. Jet-Long also generalizes to hybrid attention architectures such as Jet-Nemotron for further long-context improvement without retraining, and remains hyperparameter-resilient for ease of deployment.

</details>


### [18] [Architecture Generalization with MetaNCA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07743)
*Meet Barot,Daniel Berenberg,Sina Khajehabdollahi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Meta Neural Cellular Automata (MetaNCA)框架，通过局部规则自组织生成神经网络权重，无需反向传播即可为多种架构生成参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物神经元通过突触的局部相互作用实现高效学习和适应，受此启发，现有的神经细胞自动机(NCA)模型已成功应用于形态发生任务。本文旨在将这种基于局部交互的自组织特性扩展到神经网络权重学习领域，实现无需反向传播的网络参数生成。

Method: 提出MetaNCA框架，使用学习到的规则网络迭代更新任务网络权重，仅依赖计算图上的局部交互。设计新颖的Weight Transformer架构作为局部规则网络，利用线性注意力聚合来自相邻权重和隐藏状态的信号。训练完成后，规则网络可为多种架构生成任务网络，无需反向传播。

Result: MetaNCA成功为MNIST和CIFAR-100数据集上的前馈MLP、CNN和ResNet生成权重，可扩展至200万参数的网络规模。MetaNCA能够泛化到元训练期间未见过的架构，且训练阶段的架构多样性可增强这种泛化能力。

Conclusion: MetaNCA框架证明了通过局部规则自组织生成神经网络权重的可行性，实现了跨架构的泛化能力，为神经网络的训练提供了无需反向传播的新范式。

Abstract: Self-organization is an emergent property of life, driven by the collective behavior of individual components acting on local information. Biological neurons, through local interactions transmitted through synapses, are able to learn efficiently and can adapt their connections over an organism's lifespan. Motivated by these desirable properties of adaptability and local interaction, neural cellular automata (NCA) models have been successful at learning morphogenesis solely through local update rules, demonstrating stability over many updates and robustness to perturbations. In this work, we introduce Meta Neural Cellular Automata (MetaNCA), a framework that learns local rules which self-organize the weights of artificial neural networks. A learned rule network iteratively updates the weights of a task network using only local interactions on the computation graph. We propose a novel Weight Transformer architecture for the local rule network, which uses linear attention to aggregate signals from neighboring weights and hidden states. Once trained, the rule network generates task networks of diverse architectures without backpropagation. We show that MetaNCA generates weights for feedforward MLPs, CNNs, and ResNets on MNIST and CIFAR-100, scaling to networks of 2 million parameters. We further show that MetaNCA generalizes to architectures not seen during meta-training, and that architectural diversity in the training phase strengthens this generalization.

</details>


### [19] [Collate: Collaborative Neural Network Learning for Latency-Critical Edge Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08013)
*Shuo Huai,Di Liu,Hao Kong,Xiangzhong Luo,Weichen Liu,Ravi Subramaniam,Christian Makaya,Qian Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Collate框架，通过协同学习异构模型来同时满足多个边缘系统的延迟约束，在单一训练过程中为不同系统提供满足延迟要求且高精度的模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习在实时边缘系统部署时面临设备异构性挑战，现有优化方法主要关注训练效率而忽视了推理阶段的延迟约束，无法在保证精度的同时满足不同设备的实时性要求。

Method: 提出Collate训练框架，包含两个核心组件：(1)动态零化-恢复方法，用于调整每个本地模型架构以在延迟约束下获得高精度；(2)原型校正联邦聚合方案，用于聚合所有异构本地模型，仅需一次训练过程即可满足不同系统的延迟约束并保持高精度。

Result: 实验结果表明，在延迟约束下，扩展模型平均提高精度1.96%，缩减模型平均提高精度3.09%，且几乎没有额外的训练开销。

Conclusion: Collate框架成功解决了联邦学习在异构边缘系统中的延迟约束问题，通过单次训练为不同性能设备提供优化的模型，实现了精度与效率的良好平衡。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) empowers multiple clients to collaboratively learn a model, enlarging the training data of each client for high accuracy while protecting data privacy. However, when deploying FL in real-time edge systems, the heterogeneity of devices among systems has a severe impact on the performance of the inferred model. Existing optimizations on FL focus on improving the training efficiency but fail to speed up inference, especially when there is a latency constraint. In this work, we propose Collate, a novel training framework that collaboratively learns heterogeneous models to meet the latency constraints of multiple edge systems simultaneously. We design a dynamic zeroizing-recovering method to adjust each local model architecture for high accuracy under its latency constraint. A proto-corrected federated aggregation scheme is also introduced to aggregate all heterogeneous local models, satisfying the latency constraint of different systems with only one training process and maintaining high accuracy. Extensive experiments indicate that, compared to state-of-the-art methods and under a latency constraint, our extended models can improve the accuracy by 1.96% on average, and our shrunk models can also obtain a 3.09% accuracy improvement on average, with almost no extra training overhead. The related codes and data will be available at https://github.com/ntuliuteam/Collate

</details>


### [20] [LiST: Lipschitz Scaling Training for Robust and Calibrated Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07745)
*Arthur Chiron,Franck Mamalet,Thomas Massena,Thomas Deltort,Mathieu Serrurier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LiST训练范式，通过建立Lipschitz约束与温度缩放的理论联系，首次实现了神经网络在精度、鲁棒性和校准性三个关键指标上的同时优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的神经网络研究通常独立处理精度、鲁棒性和校准性三个关键属性，如何同时满足这三者仍是核心挑战。Lipschitz约束模型虽能保证鲁棒性，但其约束值L的手动选择影响精度-鲁棒性权衡，且其校准特性尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出Lipschitz Scaling Training (LiST)方法：1）发现Lipschitz约束与温度缩放之间存在理论联系，存在特定的L*值可直接获得校准网络；2）迭代调整全局Lipschitz常数以达到校准最优操作点；3）通过损失函数中的边界参数构建完全校准的Pareto前沿；4）收敛后将校准数据重新纳入训练以提高样本效率。

Result: 在CIFAR-10/100和Tiny-ImageNet数据集上验证表明，LiST在精度和鲁棒性方面与有约束及无约束基线模型具有竞争力，同时实现了开箱即用的良好校准性能，无需额外的校准步骤。

Conclusion: 本研究揭示了Lipschitz约束与模型校准之间的内在联系，提出了校准作为选择精度-鲁棒性权衡操作点的原则性标准。LiST为构建同时满足精度、鲁棒性和校准性的可靠神经网络提供了新的训练范式。

Abstract: While accuracy, robustness, and calibration are all essential for reliable neural networks, they are often studied separately; developing models that satisfy all three simultaneously remains a central challenge. Lipschitz-constrained models guarantee robustness by design, yet the manual selection of the Lipschitz constraint L governs the resulting accuracy-robustness trade-off, and their calibration properties remain largely underexplored. In this work, we highlight a theoretical and empirical link between the enforced Lipschitz constraint and Temperature Scaling, a state-of-the-art calibration method. Specifically, we find that for a given training scheme, there exists a non-trivial value L* that yields an out-of-the-box calibrated network, and that calibration acts as a principled criterion to select a well-defined operating point on the accuracy-robustness Pareto front. Leveraging these insights, we introduce Lipschitz Scaling Training (LiST), a novel training paradigm that iteratively adjusts the global Lipschitz constant to reach this operating point. Through a margin parameter in the training loss, LiST further enables the construction of a fully calibrated Pareto front, allowing users to navigate the accuracy-robustness trade-off while remaining calibrated throughout. At convergence, LiST also enables the reintegration of calibration data into training, improving sample efficiency without sacrificing calibration. We validate LiST on CIFAR-10/100 and Tiny-ImageNet, demonstrating competitive accuracy and robustness against constrained and unconstrained baselines, while remaining calibrated out of the box. Code is available at GitHub.

</details>


### [21] [Selective Left-Shift: Turning Test-Time Compute and Difficulty-based Curation into Training Data for Low-Resource Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07748)
*Didula Samaraweera,Anjana Supun,Srinath Perera*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出三阶段流水线解决低资源编程语言代码生成的困境：离线数据合成、监督微调和可验证强化学习，在Julia上Pass@1提升最高14.2分，成本仅需SOTA的1/6。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在Python等高资源语言上代码生成能力强，但在Julia等低资源编程语言(LRPLs)上性能急剧下降。改进小语言模型面临三难困境：监督微调受数据稀缺限制、推理时扩展部署成本过高、从头强化学习收益近乎为零。

Method: 提出三阶段流水线解耦语法获取与算法推理：(1)将推理时计算左移至离线数据合成引擎，利用迭代编译器和测试反馈生成验证过的训练样本；(2)在此合成验证数据上微调SLM以嵌入强语法先验；(3)应用基于语言无关I/O测试的可验证强化学习(RLVR)，SFT先验约束探索避免语法错误。

Result: 在Qwen3-8B上应用该方法，MultiPL-E上Pass@1提升最高+7.6分，Julia的Agnostics LiveCodeBench上提升+14.2分；仅使用SOTA方法1/3的数据和1/6的成本；在Ballerina语言上达到49.7% MultiPL-E Pass@1（该语言预训练表示近乎为零）。

Conclusion: 该流水线有效解决了LRPLs代码生成的困境，证明了语法获取与算法推理解耦的有效性。消融实验证实SFT阶段和执行验证奖励对稳定训练都是必要的，方法具有良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Large Language Models achieve strong code generation for high resource languages like Python and Java but suffer sharp performance drops on Low-Resource Programming Languages~(LRPLs) such as Julia. Improving Small Language Models~(SLMs) for these languages faces a trilemma: Supervised Fine-Tuning~(SFT) is bottlenecked by data scarcity, inference-time scaling is too expensive for deployment, and Reinforcement Learning from scratch yields near zero advantages. We propose a three-phase pipeline that resolves this trilemma by decoupling syntax acquisition from algorithmic reasoning. First, we \emph{left-shift} inference-time compute to an offline data synthesis engine that uses iterative compiler and test feedback to generate verified training examples. Second, we fine-tune an SLM on this synthetic, verified data to embed strong syntactic priors. Third, we apply Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Reward~(RLVR) grounded by language-agnostic Input/Output tests, where the SFT prior constrains exploration away from syntax errors. Applied to Qwen3-8B, our pipeline improves pass@1 by up to +7.6 points on MultiPL-E and +14.2 points on the Agnostics LiveCodeBench for Julia compared to SOTA results. Furthermore, the pipeline only used $\frac{1}{3}$ data and $\frac{1}{6}$ cost over the previous state-of-the-art. We further demonstrate that the pipeline generalizes to Ballerina achieving 49.7\% MultiPL-E Pass@1, a language with near-zero pretraining representation. Ablations confirm that both the SFT phase and execution-grounded rewards are necessary for stable training.

</details>


### [22] [Secure Decentralized Federated Learning via Gossip and Virtual Voting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08651)
*Amirhossein Taherpour,Xiaodong Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了gspDAG-FL框架，通过从模型传播的gossip历史中构建共识，实现了去中心化联邦学习的安全性、最终性和 Byzantine/lazy节点的容错性，在保持高检测率和学习质量的同时减少了协调开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的去中心化联邦学习(DFL)基于gossip的方法缺乏可追溯的最终性，且对Byzantine或lazy参与者缺乏弹性；而账本辅助的联邦学习虽然提高了可审计性，但区块链、分片或结算委员会等方法会重新引入全局协调成本，与DFL的本地性原则冲突。

Method: 提出gspDAG-FL框架，节点仅与邻居交换模型负载，全节点收集事件证书和接收方背书的已接受gossip证明，重建紧凑的拓扑有向无环图(DAG)，运行Hashgraph风格的虚拟投票并生成紧凑的全节点证书。最终性基于唯一的模型-来源元组，而非完全相同的本地参数状态。结合负载验证、已接受证明验证和私有语义审计来增强弹性。

Result: 在MNIST分类和Penn Treebank语言建模任务上，使用公平的留出验证/审计数据和最多N=100的网络规模进行实验，gspDAG-FL在学习质量上接近基于验证的账本FL，同时减少了协调瓶颈，提高了吞吐量，并在混合Byzantine和lazy参与下保持高无效来源检测率。

Conclusion: gspDAG-FL成功证明了安全性和控制平面的条件活性，为经过认证的扰动gossip提供了收敛保证，实现了在时变有效混合下的理论保证，为去中心化联邦学习提供了一个安全、高效且具有理论保障的解决方案。

Abstract: Decentralized federated learning (DFL) removes the central server by letting nodes exchange model updates through peer-to-peer gossip, but existing gossip-based methods often lack provenance finality and resilience to Byzantine or lazy participants. Ledger-assisted federated learning (FL) improves auditability, yet blockchains, shards, or settlement committees can reintroduce global coordination costs that conflict with DFL locality. This paper proposes \emph{gspDAG-FL}, a secure DFL framework that derives consensus from the same gossip history used to disseminate models. Nodes exchange model payloads only with neighbors, while full nodes collect event certificates and receiver-endorsed accepted gossip proofs, reconstruct a compact Topology directed acyclic graph (DAG), and run Hashgraph-style virtual voting followed by compact full-node certificates. Finality is over unique model-origin tuples, not identical local parameter states. To improve resilience, gspDAG-FL combines payload validation, accepted-proof validation, and private semantic audit before aggregation. We formalize the adversarial setting, prove safety and conditional liveness of the control plane, and give a convergence guarantee for certified perturbed gossip under time-varying effective mixing. Experiments on MNIST classification and Penn Treebank language modeling, using fair held-out validation/audit data and networks up to \(N=100\), show that gspDAG-FL achieves learning quality close to validation-based ledger FL while reducing coordination bottlenecks, improving throughput, and maintaining high invalid-origin detection under mixed Byzantine and lazy participation.

</details>

Result: （1）所有障碍均表现出分级单调的剂量-响应关系；（2）障碍自组织成二维情感空间，躁狂与焦虑呈镜像关系；（3）移除权重参数可缓解奖励扭曲类障碍，但回避类障碍需要分级暴露课程；（4）两个权重同时调节产生非加性交互作用，可预测共病模式；（5）框架可迁移至三维像素环境MiniWorld。

Abstract: Modelling psychological disorders in artificial agents offers both a testbed for computational psychiatry and a lens on the failure modes of affective control. Prior work induces one or two disorders in a reinforcement learning (RL) agent by hand-tuned reward shaping, labels the behaviour post hoc, and reports single runs. We recast disorder modelling as dose-controllable manipulation of cognitive appraisal signals in an appraisal-guided PPO agent, expressing seven disorders (anxiety, mania, obsessive-compulsive checking, depression, impulsivity, addiction, and post-traumatic stress) each as a single knob grounded in a computational psychiatry account, with each symptom measured by a preregistered assay mapped to a recognised paradigm. Across more than a thousand runs (10 seeds, four controls, 95% confidence intervals) every disorder shows a graded, monotone dose-response that no control reproduces. Beyond these induced effects, three findings emerge that were not written into the reward: the disorders self-organise into a two-dimensional affective space in which mania mirrors anxiety; removing a knob remits reward distortion disorders (mania, checking, addiction) but not avoidance disorders (anxiety, PTSD), which instead recover under a graded exposure curriculum; and two simultaneous knobs interact nonadditively, yielding testable comorbidity predictions. Appraisal weights thus parameterise a controllable space of affective phenotypes in which the same knobs that induce a disorder can model its treatment. We also show that three disorder knobs (depression, addiction, anxiety) transfer to a three-dimensional pixel environment (MiniWorld) with a standard convolutional agent and no appraisal critic, with cross-assay dissociation confirmed across both domains, indicating the framework is not specific to grid worlds or to PPO's appraisal critic.

</details>

*Kumari Jyoti,Rohith Babu,Apoorva D. Patel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种量子启发的混合经典-量子卷积神经网络框架，通过混合专家策略提升图像分类性能，在MNIST和Fashion-MNIST数据集上将预测失败率降低约一半，且在GPU工作站上仅带来适度的计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统卷积神经网络在图像处理中依赖扩散平滑和块状池化进行下采样，存在效率瓶颈。作者旨在探索更高效的量子启发策略，利用混合专家架构提升特征提取和分类性能。

Method: 提出混合经典-量子框架：量子部分采用振幅编码、局部酉操作卷积、多专家并行处理（不同参数）和量子稳定码特征提取；经典部分通过全连接神经网络融合多专家特征进行类别预测。

Result: 在MNIST和Fashion-MNIST数据集上，联合专家分析优于单个专家，预测失败率降低约50%，GPU工作站上的计算开销适中，验证了其实用性。

Conclusion: 该量子启发策略为现有经典方案提供了实用的替代选择，框架中的量子部分可在量子处理器上执行，展示了量子-经典混合架构在模式识别中的潜力。

Abstract: Pattern recognition problems arise in a variety of physical image processing situations, and convolutional neural networks are a popular scheme for the required feature extraction and classification tasks. The classical networks use diffusion-based smearing and block-wise pooling to downsample the image data and capture important structural features. In this work, we propose and demonstrate a more efficient quantum-inspired strategy involving a mixture of experts. It is a hybrid classical-quantum framework. The quantum part consists of amplitude encoding of the images, convolution using local unitary operations, multiple experts processing the same image with different parameters, and feature extraction using quantum stabiliser codes. The classical part then jointly processes the features extracted by different experts using a standard fully connected neural network for image class prediction. Using MNIST and Fashion-MNIST datasets as benchmarks, we demonstrate that the joint expert analysis outperforms the individual expert one, as well as reduces the failure rate of image class prediction by around a factor of two. The overhead of our quantum-inspired strategy is only moderate on GPU workstations, which makes our proposal a practical alternative to existing classical schemes. We also point out how the quantum part of our framework can be executed on a quantum processor.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
