### [84] [Systematic Evaluation of Learning Rate Scheduling Strategies Across Heterogeneous Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08511)
*Hafsa Mateen,Radu Timofte,Dmitry Ignatov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究通过大规模实验系统性地评估了学习率调度策略对神经网络分类精度的影响。在CIFAR-10数据集上，对30种代表性架构和25种调度器配置进行了3,938次实验，发现调度器选择高度依赖架构，其中CosineAnnealingWarmRestarts和CyclicLR表现最优，最佳精度达86.45%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习率调度策略的选择对神经网络训练至关重要，但人工选择成本高昂且难以穷尽。现有的经典AutoML方法通常将调度器视为次要超参数，缺乏对其影响的系统性研究。因此，本研究旨在填补这一空白，全面调查调度器对不同架构分类精度的影响。

Method: 研究基于LEMUR神经网络数据集，选取30种代表性架构（包括卷积神经网络和Transformer系列）。通过自动源代码注入技术，应用来自9个PyTorch系列的25种调度器配置，在CIFAR-10数据集上共评估了3,938个模型变体，进行大规模系统性实验。

Result: 最佳配置达到了86.45%的top-1精度，237个变体的精度超过80%。实验结果表明调度器的选择高度依赖于网络架构：CosineAnnealingWarmRestarts和CyclicLR调度器在各种架构上持续优于基础衰减策略。研究构建了精度景观并贡献到LEMUR nn-dataset数据集中。

Conclusion: 学习率调度策略的选择对模型性能有显著影响，且这种影响与网络架构密切相关。研究为基于原则的调度器选择提供了实用的参考基准，证明了系统性研究调度策略的重要价值，为神经网络训练实践提供了有价值的指导。

Abstract: Choosing a learning rate scheduling strategy is critical to neural network training, but manual selection is costly and rarely exhaustive. While classical AutoML approaches often treat the scheduler as a secondary hyperparameter, we systematically investigate its impact on classification accuracy across a diverse pool of architectures. We evaluated 30 representative architectures from convolutional and transformer families within the LEMUR neural network dataset. Through automated source-code injection, we applied 25 scheduler configurations across nine PyTorch families, evaluating a total of 3,938 model variants on CIFAR-10. Our best configuration achieved a top-1 accuracy of 86.45%, with 237 variants exceeding 80%. The results show that the choice of scheduler depends heavily on the architecture: CosineAnnealingWarmRestarts and CyclicLR consistently outperform basic decay strategies. The resulting accuracy landscape, contributed to the LEMUR nn-dataset, provides a practical reference for principled scheduler selection.

</details>


### [85] [Stop Guessing When to Stop Testing: Efficient Model Evaluation with Just Enough Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08522)
*Ofir Arviv,Kristjan Greenewald,Yotam Perlitz,Hadar Mulian,Michal Shmueli-Scheuer,Leshem Choshen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种自适应评估框架，结合序贯检验和定制停止准则，在保持统计显著性的同时大幅降低计算成本（在Open VLM排行榜上实现80%的成本降低）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定规模的基准测试存在固有刚性，无法满足模型排名、模型选择和开发测试等多样化评估目标对不同统计功效的需求，导致计算成本过高或可靠性受损的问题。

Method: 提出了一个自适应评估框架，将成熟的序贯检验统计范式与针对常见评估需求（如收益递减检测、最小可检测效应量）定制的停止准则相结合。

Result: 在Open VLM排行榜上验证了框架的有效性，在保持统计显著性的前提下，相比固定规模评估实现了80%的计算成本降低（允许2.5分的置信区间宽度）。

Conclusion: 序贯检验方法能够有效管理模型评估中效率与可靠性的权衡，为模型评估领域提供了一种原则性的自适应解决方案。

Abstract: The inherent rigidity of fixed-size benchmarks makes them an inefficient tool for model evaluation. Diverse evaluation objectives, including model ranking, model selection and testing throughout development, demand varying levels of statistical power. The mismatch between fixed sample sizes and these diverse needs results in either excessive computational cost or compromised reliability - a critical concern for model evaluation. To overcome these limitations, we call for adoption of sequential testing in our field. We provide an adaptive evaluation framework, that provides a principled way to navigate the trade-off between efficiency and reliability in model evaluation. Our framework combines the established statistical paradigm of sequential testing with stopping criteria tailored to common evaluation needs such as diminishing returns detection, and minimum detectable effect size. We demonstrate its ability to adaptively manage the efficiency-reliability trade-off on the Open VLM Leaderboard, including, for example, a 80% reduction in computational cost compared to fixed-size evaluation (with a 2.5-point CI width allowance) while maintaining statistical significance.

</details>


### [86] [CAAD: Causality-Aware Multivariate Time Series Anomaly Detection via Multi-Scale Alignment and Structural Causal Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08555)
*Xin Wang,Yunshi Wen,Yanan He,Haotian Xu,Youlan Zhao,Michel Ferreira Cardia Haddad,Tengfei Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CAAD框架，通过连续验证格兰杰因果关系一致性来进行异常检测，利用外生变量和梯度矩阵监控因果关系的破坏，在真实工业数据集上优于现有最先进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有异常检测方法主要关注时间表示的相似性，而忽视了系统故障和潜在异常的特征——即内部因果关系的破坏。工业系统故障的本质特征是因果关系的断裂或改变，而非简单的时序模式偏离，因此需要从因果视角重新审视异常检测问题。

Method: CAAD框架将异常检测重构为格兰杰因果关系一致性的连续验证过程。具体包括：(1)将外生时间序列变量建模为残差，识别由外部干预引起的显著偏差；(2)采用多尺度对齐机制内化系统动态特性；(3)利用梯度矩阵监控内部因果关系的破坏；(4)量化动态演化和关系拓扑两方面的因果偏差，从而捕获细微的因果变化。

Result: 在真实工业数据集上的大量实验表明，CAAD实现了高精度异常检测，性能优于大多数最先进的基线方法，有效验证了因果一致性验证方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过将异常检测重新定义为因果关系一致性的连续验证，CAAD框架成功捕获了表征系统故障的因果关系破坏，为复杂工业系统的异常检测提供了新的因果视角，证明了因果关系建模在异常检测任务中的重要价值。

Abstract: The operational integrity of complex industrial systems relies on precise anomaly detection and diagnosis. The vast majority of existing methods narrowly focus on capturing temporal similarities of representations, often overlooking the disruption of internal causal relationships, which characterizes system failures and latent anomalies. In this paper, we propose a novel framework (CAAD) that reframes anomaly detection as the continuous verification of Granger causality consistency through exogenous variables.
  Specifically, the CAAD framework models exogenous time-series variables as residuals, identifying anomalies as significant deviations caused by external interventions. The proposed framework leverages multi-scale alignment to internalize system dynamics and utilizes a gradient-based matrix to monitor internal causal relationship breakdowns. By quantifying causal deviations of both dynamic evolution and relational topology, the CAAD is able to capture subtle causal shifts to achieve precise anomaly detection. Extensive experiments on real-world industrial datasets demonstrate that the CAAD achieves high-precision anomaly detection, outperforming most state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [87] [Contravariance Theory: Strong Alignment for Minimal Solutions to Hard Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08561)
*Dan Yamins,Aran Nayebi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了在足够困难的任务下，深度神经网络（DNN）之间的表示对齐具有必然性，且收敛性演化不可避免，网络比较的度量选择不那么敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过去十五年中，NeuroAI领域的一系列结果引发了关于如何将深度神经网络（DNN）模型与大脑进行比较，以及人工网络与真实大脑网络之间应期望多大程度的收敛性演化的核心问题。

Method: 理论证明方法。作者针对任意两个解决足够困难任务的最小DNN解，证明了两条性质：(i) 基于仿射映射的网络表示"弱"对齐能够保证特权轴的"强"对齐；(ii) 对齐在网络层级中呈"拉链式"传递，导致特权轴从端到端任务优化中涌现。

Result: 研究发现：(1) 弱对齐（基于仿射映射）可以保证特权轴的强对齐；(2) 对齐在网络层级中逐层传递，形成"拉链效应"；(3) 这些结果形式化了Cao和Yamins [2024]提出的反变性概念。

Conclusion: 对于NeuroAI理论具有重要启示：在足够强的任务驱动下，网络间比较的度量选择并不那么敏感，且收敛性演化很可能是不可避免的。这意味着人工神经网络与生物神经网络之间会自发形成相似的结构表示。

Abstract: A series of results from the NeuroAI over the past fifteen years have raised core questions both about how to compare Deep Neural Network (DNN) models to the brain, and about how much convergent evolution to expect between artificial networks and real brain networks. Here, we show that for any two minimal DNN solutions to a sufficiently hard task: (i) "weak" alignment of network representations based on affine mappings guarantees "strong" alignment of privileged axes, and (ii) alignment "zippers" up the network hierarchy, causing the emergence of privileged axes from end-to-end task optimization. These results formalize the notion of contravariance from Cao and Yamins [2024], and illustrate important consequences for the theory of NeuroAI: with sufficiently strong tasks, choice of metric for inter-network comparison is not all that sensitive, and that convergent evolution is probably inevitable.

</details>


### [88] [Spectral Stability of Pseudoinverse-Based Extreme Learning Machine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08581)
*Bich Van Nguyen,Ngoc Anh Khong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从谱分析角度研究ELM的数值稳定性问题，发现最小奇异值控制输出权重的扰动放大，条件数量化隐藏层不稳定性；实验表明SVD方法在病态条件下最可靠，迭代方法对谱性质更敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极限学习机（ELM）使用Moore-Penrose伪逆解析计算输出权重，训练速度快，但其数值稳定性严重依赖于隐藏层矩阵的条件数。现有方法缺乏对ELM稳定性的系统性谱分析，特别是在病态条件下的表现。

Method: 从谱分析视角研究基于伪逆的ELM：分析最小奇异值对输出权重扰动放大的影响；使用条件数量化隐藏层不稳定性；比较SVD伪逆计算与迭代超幂方法；通过随机特征解释分析宽度相关的条件问题。

Result: 理论分析表明最小奇异值控制输出权重的扰动放大，条件数提供隐藏层不稳定性的定量度量。实验结果显示：SVD方法在病态条件下保持最可靠，迭代方法对谱性质更敏感；ELM稳定性根本由隐藏层矩阵的奇异值结构决定。

Conclusion: ELM的数值稳定性本质上由隐藏层矩阵的奇异值结构决定。在病态条件下，SVD方法比迭代方法更可靠，这为ELM的实际应用和数值实现提供了重要指导。

Abstract: Extreme Learning Machine (ELM) computes output weights analytically using the Moore-Penrose pseudoinverse. Although this leads to fast training, its numerical stability depends strongly on the conditioning of the hidden layer matrix. This paper studies pseudoinverse-based ELM from a spectral perspective. We show that the smallest singular value governs perturbation amplification in the output weights, while the condition number provides a quantitative measure of hidden-layer instability. We compare SVD-based pseudoinverse computation with iterative hyperpower methods and discuss width-dependent conditioning through a random feature interpretation. Experiments on synthetic matrices and ELM benchmarks show that SVD-based methods remain the most reliable under ill conditioning, while iterative methods are more sensitive to spectral properties. The results suggest that ELM stability is fundamentally governed by the singular value structure of the hidden layer matrix.

</details>


### [89] [Robust Bayesian Decision Making under Adversarial Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08590)
*Haripriya Harikumar,Sammie Katt,Yasir Zubayr Barlas,Samuel Kaski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了对抗鲁棒的决策感知实验设计方法，通过考虑最坏情况下的意外影响，使实验设计目标从名义最优性转向决策稳定性，从而在存在隐藏或弱建模效应的情况下产生更可靠的决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有决策感知实验设计和主动学习方法通常假设结果模型规范良好，并隐式依赖于现实世界扰动下最优决策的稳定性。然而在实践中，实验结果经常受到隐藏或弱建模效应的影响，这会显著改变决策最优性并导致误导性结论。传统方法可能在存在意外效应时产生脆弱的高置信度决策。

Method: 研究序贯对抗鲁棒决策感知实验设计问题，将数据获取过程考虑为对抗合理最坏情况下的意外效应，并将其建模为对抗变量的变化。基于贝叶斯决策理论，形式化了该设置下的对抗鲁棒最优决策，并推导出一个原则性的贝叶斯实验设计准则，该准则明确针对决策稳定性而非名义最优性。

Result: 在合成和真实科学数据集上的实验表明，传统的决策感知设计可以快速收敛到高置信度但脆弱的决策，而本文提出的鲁棒感知方法在对抗变化下产生显著更稳定和可靠的决策。

Conclusion: 通过将实验设计目标从信息增益最大化转向支持可靠的下游决策，并明确考虑对抗性变化下的最坏情况，所提出的方法能够产生比传统方法更稳定的决策，为实际科学实验中的决策制定提供了更可靠的框架。

Abstract: Scientific experiments are often designed to maximize information gain, yet in many applications the primary objective is to support reliable downstream decision-making. Existing decision-aware experimental design and active learning methods typically assume well-specified outcome models and implicitly rely on the stability of the optimal decision under real-world perturbations. In practice, however, experimental outcomes are frequently influenced by hidden or weakly modeled effects, which can substantially alter decision optimality and lead to misleading conclusions. We study sequential adversarially robust decision-aware experimental design, where data acquisition has to take into account information gain against plausible worst-case unexpected effects, modeled here as variation in adversarial variables. Building on Bayesian decision theory, we formalize an adversarially robust optimal decision under this setting and derive a principled Bayesian experimental design criterion. The criterion explicitly targets decision stability rather than nominal optimality. Experiments on synthetic and real-world scientific datasets show that conventional decision-aware design can converge rapidly to high confidence yet fragile decisions, while our robustness-aware approach yields decisions that are significantly more stable and reliable under adversarial variation.

</details>


### [90] [Federated Deep Learning for Privacy-Preserving Cardiovascular Disease Risk Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08595)
*Hyunho Mo,Djura Smits,Mahlet A. Birhanu,Maarten J. G. Leening,Daniel Bos,Pim van der Harst,Esther E. Bron*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种隐私保护的联邦深度学习方法，通过整合两个特征不同的队列（Lifelines和鹿特丹研究），在不共享患者个体数据的情况下提高了心血管疾病风险预测性能，C统计量分别提升至0.739和0.787。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心血管疾病风险预测模型通常依赖单一机构或集中式数据集。跨机构扩展这些模型受到隐私法规和患者数据共享限制。联邦学习可在不传输敏感患者数据的情况下实现协作模型开发，但在医疗保健中的应用仍具挑战性，因为数据集在规模、人群特征和结果定义方面存在差异。

Method: 提出一种隐私保护的联邦深度学习方法用于心血管疾病风险预测，整合了两个人群队列：包含148,230名参与者的Lifelines队列（自我报告结果）和包含10,155名参与者的鹿特丹研究（数字化链接的临床结果）。采用深度生存模型进行联邦学习训练，主要在鹿特丹研究数据集上进行性能评估。

Result: 联邦学习训练的深度生存模型比本地训练（无联邦）模型具有更高的预测性能。鹿特丹研究的C统计量从0.728（95% CI: 0.717-0.739）提高到0.739（95% CI: 0.728-0.749）；Lifelines的C统计量从0.783（95% CI: 0.775-0.791）提高到0.787（95% CI: 0.780-0.792）。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，跨异质队列的联邦深度学习可以在保护个体患者数据隐私的同时，改善心血管疾病风险预测性能，为隐私保护下的多中心协作建模提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Cardiovascular disease risk prediction models often rely on data from a single institution or centrally pooled datasets. Extending these models across institutions could be limited by privacy regulations and constraints on sharing patient-level data. Federated learning enables collaborative model development without transferring sensitive patient data, but its application in healthcare remains challenging because datasets often differ in size, population characteristics, and outcome definitions. In this study, we present a federated deep learning approach for privacy-preserving cardiovascular disease risk prediction that integrates two population-based cohorts with different characteristics: Lifelines, including 148,230 participants meeting the study inclusion criteria with self-reported outcomes, and the Rotterdam Study, including a smaller cohort of 10,155 participants with digitally linked clinical outcomes. Model performance was primarily evaluated on the Rotterdam Study because of its complete follow-up. Deep survival models trained using federated learning achieved higher predictive performance than models trained locally without federation. For the Rotterdam Study, the C-statistic increased from 0.728 (95% CI: 0.717-0.739) to 0.739 (95% CI: 0.728-0.749). For Lifelines, the C-statistic increased from 0.783 (95% CI: 0.775-0.791) to 0.787 (95% CI: 0.780-0.792). These findings suggest that federated deep learning across heterogeneous cohorts can improve cardiovascular disease risk prediction while preserving the privacy of individual-level patient data.

</details>


### [91] [Steering Neural Network Training through Interpretable Constraints Based on Partial Dependence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08641)
*Yann Claes,Pierre Geurts,Vân Anh Huynh-Thu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于偏依赖的解释引导学习方法，通过约束神经网络使其响应与领域知识对齐，在回归问题上提升了模型性能和数据效率，并保证了模型解释与先验知识的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究大多关注如何解释模型交互，而较少关注解释质量评估和如何调整模型使解释符合先验知识（解释引导学习），且现有方法主要针对分类问题并假设已知重要特征或区域。

Method: 提出了一种基于偏依赖的神经网络引导方法，通过对特定特征的平均响应施加约束，使模型输出与问题的功能领域知识保持一致。

Result: 在包括动力系统预测在内的多个回归问题上的实验表明，该方法约束下的模型性能优于无约束模型，且具有更高的数据效率；模型解释结果与用户提供的知识相一致，而无约束模型的解释则不符。

Conclusion: 基于偏依赖的解释引导学习框架能够有效整合领域知识到神经网络中，不仅提升了回归任务的预测性能和数据效率，还确保了模型解释与先验知识的忠实对齐。

Abstract: Over the last few years, there has been an increased interest in making machine learning models more interpretable. Although a great deal of effort goes into developing techniques for interpreting the interactions learned by a given model, fewer studies focus on assessing the quality of such explanations. Even fewer focus on how to adjust the model to produce explanations faithful to prior knowledge, a process known as explanation-guided learning. Furthermore, most approaches in this area focus on classification problems and usually assume prior knowledge about which input features or regions are most important. In this work, we introduce a new approach to steering neural networks based on partial dependence, such that their average response to certain features aligns with specific functional domain knowledge about the problem. We empirically demonstrate on a range of regression problems, including dynamical systems forecasting, that models whose training has been controlled using our method perform better than unconstrained models and are more data-efficient. Moreover, we highlight that interpretations obtained from the former actually align with the user-provided knowledge, whereas those obtained from the latter do not.

</details>


### [92] [BiSCo-LLM: Lookup-Free Binary Spherical Coding for Extreme Low-Bit Large Language Model Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08643)
*Yuantian Shao,Peisong Wang,Zhilei Liu,Chuangyi Li,Yuanteng Chen,Pengcheng Xie,Yiwu Yao,Zhihui Wei,Jian Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了BiSCo-LLM，一种无码本的二进制球面编码框架，用于大语言模型的极低比特权重压缩。通过将权重映射到单位超球面并二值化、残差编码以及类别级恢复蒸馏，在无需显式码本的情况下实现高效压缩，解决了现有方法在极低比特率下表示能力受限或存储开销大的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在部署时受到内存容量、权重带宽和检查点存储的限制。现有的低比特压缩方法存在两难：标量或分组量化在目标预算接近2比特时表示能力受限；向量量化虽然表示能力更强，但需要显式码本、索引查找和额外存储开销。因此需要一种既能实现极低比特压缩，又能避免码本存储开销的方法。

Method: BiSCo-LLM框架包含三个核心组件：(1)局部权重块映射到单位超球面并二值化为紧凑的球面码，主要负载是位打包的符号流而非显式VQ质心；(2)残差BSQ阶段编码基础球面编解码器留下的重建误差，提供无需存储码本的显式率失真路径；(3)在替换每个Transformer模块类别后执行类别级恢复蒸馏，减少局部权重重建与组装模型行为之间的不匹配。此外，使用小型8位保护通道路径作为敏感通道的辅助稳定机制。

Result: 成功实现了无码本的极低比特权重压缩，避免了向量量化方法中显式码本、索引查找和额外存储开销的问题。报告的存储预算包括二进制码、神经解码器、保护通道负载、LoRA适配器和元数据，提供了完整的压缩解决方案。

Conclusion: BiSCo-LLM为大语言模型的极低比特权重压缩提供了一种有效的无码本解决方案，通过球面编码和残差编码的结合，在保持表示能力的同时避免了传统向量量化方法的存储开销，为大语言模型的部署提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly constrained by memory capacity, weight bandwidth, and checkpoint storage during deployment. Existing low-bit compression methods mainly follow two directions. Scalar or group-wise quantization is simple and compatible with efficient low-precision kernels, but its representation capacity becomes limited when the target budget approaches 2 bits per weight. Vector-quantized weight compression provides a richer block-level representation, but usually introduces explicit codebooks, index lookup, and additional storage accounting. This paper presents BiSCo-LLM, a codebook-free binary spherical coding framework for extreme low-bit LLM weight compression. The core pipeline is built on three components. First, local weight chunks are mapped onto a unit hypersphere and binarized into compact spherical codes, so that the main payload is a bit-packed sign stream rather than explicit VQ centroids. Second, a residual BSQ stage encodes the reconstruction error left by the base spherical codec, providing an explicit rate-distortion path without stored codebooks. Third, category-wise recovery distillation is performed after replacing each Transformer module category, reducing the mismatch between local weight reconstruction and assembled model behavior. A small 8-bit protected-channel path is used as an auxiliary stabilization mechanism for sensitive channels and is counted separately from the BSQ payload. The reported storage budget includes binary codes, neural decoders, protected-channel payloads, LoRA adapters, and metadata.

</details>


### [93] [Multi-Modal, Multi-Environment Machine Teaching for Robust Reward Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08647)
*Ali Larian,Qian Lin,Chang Zong Wu,Daniel S. Brown*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种跨多环境的层次化机器教学算法，通过智能选择环境和查询多模态反馈，学习到更具泛化性的奖励函数，显著降低了遗憾值并提升了在未见环境中的泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着自主代理在多样化操作场景中的部署，现有的逆强化学习(IRL)最优教学方法局限于单一环境、仅演示的设置。由于单一MDP中的演示会将奖励信息与环境特定结构纠缠，导致学习到的奖励函数在新环境中泛化能力差，因此需要研究多环境、多模态反馈下的奖励学习方法。

Method: 提出了一个跨多MDP的层次化机器教学算法：(1)理论上分析了不同反馈模态对奖励的约束，证明在无限数据情况下比较模态施加的全局约束严格强于其他模态；(2)贪婪地选择能够揭示互补奖励约束的信息性环境；(3)在选定环境中策略性地查询低成本的反馈类型。

Result: 理论分析表明比较模态在无限数据情况下施加了比其他模态严格更强的全局约束；实验证明该方法在相同反馈预算下，相比均匀教学基线实现了显著更低的遗憾值和更强的对未见环境的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 多环境、多模态教学对于学习动态鲁棒的奖励函数至关重要，该方法为如何在多样化操作场景中学习泛化性强的奖励函数提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: As autonomous agents are increasingly deployed across diverse operational contexts, aligning their behavior with human intent demands reward functions that remain robust to such changes rather than overfitting to any single environment. Inverse reinforcement learning (IRL) provides a principled way to infer such objectives from human feedback. However, existing analyses of optimal teaching approaches for IRL focus on single-environment, demonstration-only settings, leaving underexplored how heterogeneous feedback modalities and environment dynamics jointly constrain reward functions that generalize across multiple environments. Because demonstrations in one MDP entangle reward information with that environments specific structure, the resulting rewards frequently fail to generalize when the agent is deployed in a new setting. We first analyze how different feedback modalities constrain rewards, showing that, in the unlimited-data regime, comparisons impose strictly stronger global constraints than other modalities. Beyond this theoretical analysis, we introduce a hierarchical machine teaching algorithm for reward learning that operates across multiple MDPs. The algorithm first greedily selects informative environments that expose complementary reward constraints, then strategically queries low-cost feedback within those environments. Empirically, our method achieves substantially lower regret and stronger generalization to held-out environments than uniform teaching baselines under identical feedback budgets, demonstrating the importance of multi-environment, multi-modal teaching for learning dynamics-robust reward functions.

</details>


### [94] [EdgeRefine: Privacy-Utility Balance for Graphs via Jaccard Sampling under Edge Differential Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08659)
*Wenxiu Ding,Muzhi Liu,Zheng Yan,Mingjun Wang,Yifan Zhao,Qiao Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了EdgeRefine框架，通过自适应边细化技术改善图神经网络中边级差分隐私的隐私-效用权衡，在保证隐私的同时显著提升了模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图神经网络在隐私敏感领域的应用面临挑战，因为图结构可能泄露敏感连接信息。现有的边级差分隐私方法通过向邻接矩阵注入噪声来保护隐私，但更强的隐私保护需要更多噪声，导致效用大幅下降，隐私-效用平衡成为实际应用的主要障碍。

Method: EdgeRefine采用局部差分隐私框架，首先利用Jaccard相似度估计边存在概率并对边进行噪声边移除排序；然后使用隐私预算ε确定真假边比例，基于概率排名分别采样；最后通过独立的采样率k控制边的总数，确保最终图的稀疏性和可靠性。

Result: 在隐私预算ε=2.5下，EdgeRefine在ACM数据集上使用GAT时节点分类准确率比最先进基线提升17.8%，在Cora数据集上使用GCN时提升19.7%。图分类任务中，相比无噪声基线的平均准确率下降仅约5%。在图重构攻击下，所有隐私预算下相对绝对误差均保持在1以上（Cora平均1.962，AMAP平均1.472），展现出强隐私保护能力。

Conclusion: EdgeRefine成功实现了隐私保护与模型效用之间的良好平衡，在多个数据集和GNN架构上表现优异，为隐私敏感场景下的图学习提供了实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) have shown considerable success in learning from graph-structured data, but their use in privacy-sensitive areas remains difficult because graph structure can leak sensitive link information. To satisfy edge-level differential privacy, a common approach is to inject noise into all elements of the graph's adjacency matrix, thereby obfuscating the existence of any single edge. However, stronger privacy requires more noise, and excessive noise reduces utility, making the privacy-utility balance a major barrier to practical privacy-preserving graph learning.
  To address this issue, we propose EdgeRefine, a local differential privacy framework that improves this trade-off through adaptive edge refinement. EdgeRefine first estimates edge-existence probabilities using Jaccard similarity and ranks edges for noisy edge removal. To ensure the sparsity and reliability of the final graph, it uses the privacy budget $ε$ to determine the ratio of true to false edges, samples them separately based on this probability ranking, and controls the total number of edges with a separate sampling rate $k$. Extensive experiments show that EdgeRefine achieves accuracy comparable to the noise-free baseline and substantially outperforms other privacy-preserving methods across datasets and GNN architectures. Under privacy budget $ε= 2.5$, EdgeRefine improves node classification accuracy over state-of-the-art baselines by 17.8\% on ACM under GAT and 19.7\% on Cora under GCN. In graph classification, it achieves an average accuracy degradation of around 5\% compared to the noise-free baseline. Under graph reconstruction attacks, EdgeRefine maintains relative absolute error levels above 1 across all privacy budgets, averaging 1.962 on Cora and 1.472 on AMAP, indicating strong resilience against privacy leakage.

</details>


### [95] [Resample or Reroute? Budget-Aware Test-Time Model Selection for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08665)
*Teng-Ruei Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出预算感知的测试时模型选择方法RoR（Resample-or-Reroute），在有限预算下动态分配资源用于重采样或重路由，以最大化预期正确性。实验表明该方法在成本-质量帕累托前沿上优于多种基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM路由方法存在局限性：单一提交路由器无法捕获实例级选择空间；现有测试时重采样方法依赖理想化预言机（具备正确性标签和无限制预算），而实际部署系统既无正确性标签也受预算约束。此前研究未将重采样已提交模型与重路由到替代模型视为对单次查询预算的竞争性使用。

Method: 提出预算感知测试时模型选择框架：给定单次查询预算和不完美验证器，在重采样与重路由之间分配预算单位以最大化预期正确性。设计在线Resample-or-Reroute（RoR）分配策略，基于单位成本的估计边际正确率驱动决策，其行为基于选择与采样之间的可恢复性不对称性。

Result: 在四类难度不同的基准测试上，使用十一模型开放权重池的多抽取正确性张量进行回放实验。RoR策略相比单路由、单提交路由器、预算感知Best-of-K、级联和随机分配基线获得更优的成本-质量帕累托前沿，在最异构基准上收益最大。消融实验显示收益受验证器质量约束，随验证器质量下降而缩减。在提供商价格向量和无标签一致性验证器下的鲁棒性回放界定了结论的适用范围。

Conclusion: RoR策略成功实现了预算感知的测试时模型选择，在有限预算和不完美验证器条件下有效平衡重采样与重路由，优于现有方法。验证器质量是关键因素，结论在特定条件下成立，为实际部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Routing among large language models (LLMs) trades response quality against serving cost, motivated by the reported gap between deployed routers and a per-instance oracle. Recent analysis shows that test-time resampling can recover per-instance selection headroom that no single-commit router captures; however, that guarantee holds only under an idealized oracle equipped with correctness labels and an unconstrained budget, neither of which a deployed system has. To the best of our knowledge, no previous work treats resampling the committed model and rerouting to an alternative model as competing uses of a single per-query cost budget. Therefore, this work formulates budget-aware test-time model selection: given a per-query budget and an imperfect verifier, allocate each unit of budget between resampling and rerouting so that expected correctness is maximized. An online resample-or-reroute (RoR) allocation policy driven by estimated marginal correctness per unit cost is proposed, and its behavior is grounded in the recoverability asymmetry between selection and sampling. Replay experiments on newly regenerated multi-draw correctness tensors from an eleven-model open-weight pool over four benchmarks of differing difficulty show that the proposed RoR policy attains a favorable cost-quality Pareto front relative to single-route, one-commit-router, budget-aware best-of-K, cascade, and random-allocation baselines for the tested pools, with the largest gains on the most heterogeneous benchmark; an ablation further shows the gains are verifier-gated, shrinking as verifier quality degrades, and robustness replays under a provider price vector and a label-free agreement verifier delineate where the conclusions carry over.

</details>


### [96] [A Practical Investigation of Training-free Relaxed Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08690)
*Guoxuan Xia,Luka Ribar,Paul Balanca*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究并基准测试了无训练松弛推测解码技术，发现松弛方法需要更多能力评估且不适合轻量级专用草稿模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准推测解码虽然无损保留LLM采样分布，但严格保证可能限制进一步加速。近期研究提出放松这一保证可获得更快速度和可控的能力-速度权衡，但缺乏对这些无训练松弛方法的系统实证研究。

Method: 统一现有松弛推测解码方法到共享框架下，在当代设置上进行基准测试，对多种无训练松弛技术进行实证比较分析。

Result: 松弛方法需要相当大的能力评估（不同于无损推测解码），且许多松弛方法依赖草稿模型本身是好的语言模型，使其不适用于轻量级专用多token预测草稿器。

Conclusion: 为实践者提供了松弛推测解码技术的经验发现和指导原则，明确了其局限性和适用场景。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates sampling from an autoregressive LLM by using a faster auxiliary model to draft tokens which are then verified in parallel by the LLM. Standard speculative decoding is lossless: its rejection and resampling steps exactly preserve the LLM's sampling distribution. Recent work argues that relaxing this strict guarantee can yield further speed-ups, controlled capability-speed trade-offs, or even capability gains. We practically investigate training-free relaxed speculative decoding techniques, unify existing approaches within a shared framework, benchmark them on contemporary settings, and distil takeaways and empirical findings for practitioners. Important takeaways include: relaxation can require considerable capability evaluation unlike lossless speculative decoding, and many relaxed approaches rely on a drafter that is a good language model, making them unsuited for lightweight dedicated multi-token-prediction drafters.

</details>


### [97] [MPFlow: Learning Budgeted Max-Flow Optimization on the Lightning Network with Deep Graph Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08703)
*Harrison Rush,Vincent Davis,Simone Antonelli,Vikash Singh,Jesse Shrader,Emanuele Rossi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于图强化学习的方法，用于在比特币闪电网络中解决流动性放置问题，即在预算约束下选择开通哪些通道以最大化路由容量。该方法已在生产环境中部署，执行了4640个通道开通决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在比特币闪电网络中，节点运营者面临一个关键问题：在有限的预算下，应该选择开通哪些通道才能最大化其路由能力？这是一个预算约束下的组合优化问题，直接影响节点的盈利能力和网络的整体效率。

Method: 将问题建模为图上的预算约束组合优化问题，选择k条边添加以最大化s-t最大流（作为路由容量的理论度量）。采用图强化学习方法，结合消息传递策略网络、近端策略优化（PPO）和动作掩码。引入枢纽排除课程学习策略：在训练子图中移除网络的顶级枢纽节点，迫使策略学习容量感知的放置策略而非简单的枢纽依附。

Result: 在真实闪电网络快照的大规模实验中，该方法在最大流目标上持续优于强启发式基线，在多个随机种子和未见过的图上都表现优异。该方法已部署到生产环境中用于对等节点推荐，执行了4640个通道开通决策，累计分配267.3 BTC（超过1600万美元），管理30个节点。

Conclusion: 本文成功地将预算约束的流动性放置问题形式化为组合优化问题，并通过图强化学习提出了有效的解决方案。枢纽排除课程学习策略有效避免了策略过度依赖枢纽节点，生产环境的成功部署验证了方法的实用价值。

Abstract: We address liquidity placement in the Bitcoin Lightning Network (LN): given a fixed budget, which channels should a node open to maximize its routing capacity? We cast this as a budget-constrained combinatorial optimization problem on graphs, selecting $k$ edge additions that maximize $s$--$t$ max-flow, a theory-grounded measure of routing capacity, and solve it with graph reinforcement learning. Our lightweight agent combines a message-passing policy network with proximal policy optimization (PPO) and action masking, and is trained under a hub-exclusion curriculum: the network's top hubs are removed from training subgraphs, forcing the policy to learn capacity-aware placement rather than hub attachment. In extensive experiments on real Lightning Network snapshots, our method consistently outperforms strong heuristic baselines on the max-flow objective across multiple seeds and unseen graphs. The agent has been deployed in production for peer recommendations, executing 4640 channel-open decisions that cumulatively allocate 267.3 BTC over $16 million across 30 managed nodes.

</details>


### [98] [Deep Learning for Joint Narrowband Interference Cancellation and Soft Demodulation in OFDM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08717)
*Emmanouil Kavvousanos,Francky Catthoor,Vassilis Paliouras*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种统一的深度学习框架，联合解决OFDM系统中的窄带干扰消除与鲁棒软解调问题，消除了传统方法中的误码平台效应，在强干扰下性能接近最优迭代基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 窄带干扰（NBI）严重降低OFDM系统性能，导致子载波损坏和传统软解调失效。传统压缩感知（CS）抑制方法存在高顺序延迟，且产生结构化非高斯残余，导致对数似然比（LLR）不可靠、解码器饱和以及采用传统高斯映射器时出现严重误码平台。

Method: 提出统一的深度学习框架，包含两个核心网络：（1）NBI-CNet：采用物理信息卷积架构估计NBI参数并在单次前向传播中消除多音干扰，无需先验已知活跃干扰源数量；（2）LLR-CNet：作为结构白化器，将非高斯抑制后残余映射为良好校准的软度量。

Result: NBI-CNet相比最先进EOMP-IDS算法降低计算复杂度达60%（N=2048, Q=64）；在强干扰（SIR=-10 dB）下，该流水线在目标BLER=10^-4时与最优迭代基线仅相差0.2至0.5 dB SNR裕度；在轻度干扰（SIR=10 dB）且严重频谱重叠（Q=12）条件下，相比传统贪婪算法误减有效数据分量导致有效载荷损坏的问题，NBI-CNet避免了信号峰值混淆，提供超过3 dB的编码增益。

Conclusion: 该架构规避了由干扰源估计误差引发的2×10^-4误码平台，其尺度不变设计能够在无需重新训练的情况下实现对任意FFT尺寸的鲁棒泛化，有效解决了传统干扰抑制与软解调之间的流水线不匹配问题。

Abstract: Narrowband interference (NBI) severely degrades orthogonal frequency-division multiplexing (OFDM) systems by corrupting subcarriers and rendering classical soft demodulation ineffective. Conventional compressed-sensing (CS) mitigation exhibits high sequential latency and leaves structured, non-Gaussian residuals that cause log-likelihood ratio (LLR) unreliability, decoder saturation, and severe error floors when employing classical Gaussian demappers. We resolve this pipeline mismatch using a unified deep learning framework for joint NBI cancellation and robust soft demodulation. First, NBI-CNet employs a physics-informed convolutional architecture to estimate NBI parameters and remove multi-tone interference in a single forward pass. Without requiring prior knowledge of the active interferer count, NBI-CNet reduces computational complexity by up to 60% ($N{=}2048, Q{=}64$) compared to the state-of-the-art EOMP-IDS algorithm. Second, LLR-CNet acts as a structural whitener by mapping non-Gaussian post-mitigation residuals onto well-calibrated soft metrics. Simulations demonstrate that this joint framework eliminates the error floors inherent to traditional baselines across dense grids. Under severe interference ($\text{SIR}{=}{-}10$ dB), the pipeline operates within a $0.2$ to $0.5$ dB SNR margin of the optimal iterative baseline at a target block error rate (BLER) of $10^{-4}$. Under mild interference ($\text{SIR}{=}10$ dB) with heavy spectral overlap ($Q{=}12$), where classical greedy algorithms erroneously subtract valid data components and corrupt the payload, NBI-CNet avoids signal-peak confusion to deliver a coding gain exceeding $3$ dB. Finally, the architecture circumvents the $2{\times}10^{-4}$ error floor triggered by interferer-estimation errors, while its scale-invariant design enables robust generalization across arbitrary FFT sizes without retraining.

</details>


### [99] [Dimensionality Reduction Meets Network Science: Sensemaking on UMAP's kNN Graph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08746)
*Duen Horng Chau,Donghao Ren,Fred Hohman,Dominik Moritz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文挖掘UMAP内部构建的k近邻图（kNN图）的潜力，证明其蕴含的高维数据流形信息可被图算法（如PageRank、k-core分解、聚类系数等）有效利用，从而辅助数据理解，效果可与专门设计的方法相媲美或形成互补。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有UMAP工作流通常只关注其低维嵌入结果，忽略了UMAP内部构建的kNN图。该图编码了原始高维空间中的数据流形信息，不包含2D投影带来的失真，因此蕴含了丰富的、未被充分利用的结构信息。

Method: 作者提出直接利用UMAP内部的kNN图进行图分析。具体应用了三种标准图算法：（1）使用PageRank识别代表性数据点；（2）使用k-core分解揭示密集核心区域与稀疏边缘；（3）使用聚类系数检测具有高度相似数据点的紧密邻域。

Result: 在MNIST和Fashion MNIST数据集上的定量和定性评估表明，这些基于图的分析方法切实可行，且在效果上与专门设计的方法（如用于样本选择的k-medoids、用于密度聚类的HDBSCAN）相当或可形成互补。

Conclusion: UMAP内部构建的kNN图是一个被低估的资源，标准图算法可将其应用于数据感知任务（如代表点选择、密度分析、邻域检测），这为探索高维数据提供了一个更全面、更强大的工作流。

Abstract: While UMAP is widely used for exploring high-dimensional data, typical workflows focus on its lower-dimensional embedding, largely overlooking the rich k-nearest-neighbor (kNN) graph that UMAP constructs internally. This graph encodes the data manifold in its original high-dimensional space, before the distortion that UMAP's 2D projection introduces. We demonstrate the untapped potential of this internal representation, showing how standard graph algorithms applied to this graph enhance data sensemaking: (1) PageRank identifies representative data points, (2) k-core decomposition reveals dense core regions versus sparse periphery, and (3) clustering coefficient detects tight-knit neighborhoods with highly-similar data points. Through quantitative and qualitative evaluation on MNIST and Fashion MNIST, we show that these graph-based analyses are not only practical but also competitive with or complementary to purpose-built methods (e.g., k-medoids for exemplar selection, HDBSCAN for density-based clustering).

</details>


### [100] [Latent Memory Palace: Reasoning for Control as Autoregressive Variational Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08724)
*Chuning Zhu,Eva Xu,Jose Barreiros,Krishnan Srinivasan,Paarth Shah,Abhishek Gupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了潜在记忆宫殿（LMP）方法，通过自回归潜在空间的变分推理框架实现控制策略的自适应推理，在仿真和真实环境中表现优异，并可生成可变长度动作分词器提升下游策略性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类决策具有高度灵活性——有些动作立即执行，有些需要长时间思考。语言模型已展现出类似的自适应推理能力，但将其迁移到连续控制策略具有挑战性，因为直接在语言空间推理可能缺乏空间理解和精确运动所需的粒度。

Method: 提出潜在记忆宫殿（LMP），将推理表述为具有自回归潜在分布的变分推理问题，组织信息在类似记忆宫殿的自回归潜在空间中，其中检索是迭代和自适应的。推导出潜在空间强化学习技术来优化其变分下界。

Result: 生成的策略LMP-π在仿真和真实世界领域中取得强劲的实证性能，同时展现出可解释的、自适应的测试时计算分配。同样的框架产生了可变长度动作分词器LMP-tok，显著提升了下游自回归策略的性能。

Conclusion: 通过变分推理的视角，为控制中的潜在推理提供了新的视角，展示了推理能力可以通过在自回归潜在空间中组织信息而涌现。

Abstract: Human decision-making is highly flexible -- some actions are taken immediately; others require longer deliberation. Language models have exhibited a similar capacity for adaptive "reasoning." However, transferring this capability to continuous control policies has been challenging, as directly reasoning in language space may lack the granularity for spatial understanding and precise motions. In this work, we show that reasoning for control policies can emerge by organizing information in an autoregressive latent space reminiscent of a memory palace, where retrieval is iterative and adaptive. Our method, Latent Memory Palace (LMP), formulates reasoning as variational inference with an autoregressive latent distribution. We derive a latent-space reinforcement learning technique to tractably optimize its variational lower bound. The resulting policy, LMP-$π$, achieves strong empirical performance in simulation and real-world domains while exhibiting interpretable, adaptive allocation of test-time compute. We further show that the same framework yields a variable-length action tokenizer, LMP-$\texttt{tok}$, which significantly improves the performance of downstream autoregressive policies. Together, these results present a new perspective on latent reasoning for control through the lens of variational inference.

</details>


### [101] [SLORR: Simple and Efficient In-Training Low-Rank Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08754)
*David González-Martínez,Shiwei Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了SLORR框架，一种简单、无状态、保持架构的训练时低秩正则化方法，能在几乎不增加训练开销的情况下显著提升神经网络的可压缩性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的低秩分解方法在激进压缩时会导致精度损失，而现有的训练时低秩正则化方法存在需要大量SVD计算、修改模型架构引入额外参数、或依赖有状态缓存等局限性。

Method: 提出了SLORR框架，包含基于Hoyer稀疏度量和核范数的两个变体。该方法直接对原始权重矩阵进行正则化，使用GPU友好的近似方法实现正则化的前向和反向传播，并提供了近似保证。框架具有简单、无状态、保持架构完整性的特点。

Result: 在ImageNet-1K上的实验表明，SLORR在ResNet-50、ViT-B/16、ViT-L/16的短时持续训练和ResNet-18预训练中，训练开销增加不到8%即可提升压缩性。在135M和560M规模的LLM预训练中，SLORR-Hoyer平均训练开销增加不到1%，且压缩后模型性能保持显著优于未正则化模型。

Conclusion: SLORR成功解决了现有低秩正则化方法的局限性，在几乎不增加训练开销的情况下有效提升了模型的可压缩性，为神经网络压缩提供了一个实用且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Low-rank factorization is widely used to compress neural networks, but modern models are often not naturally amenable to aggressive factorization without significant accuracy loss. Existing training-time low-rank regularizers can improve compressibility, but they often require SVDs of large weight matrices, modify the model architecture (introducing additional trainable parameters), or rely on stateful cached quantities. To address these limitations, we introduce SLORR, a simple, stateless, and architecture-preserving framework for in-training low-rank regularization, instantiated with two main variants based on the Hoyer sparsity metric and the nuclear norm. SLORR directly regularizes the original weight matrices using GPU-friendly approximations for the forward and backward passes of the regularizers, for which we provide approximation guarantees. We first evaluate SLORR on ImageNet-1K across short-horizon continued training of ResNet-50, ViT-B/16, and ViT-L/16, and pretraining of ResNet-18, where SLORR induces compressibility while introducing less than 8% training overhead. We further evaluate SLORR-Hoyer in LLM pretraining at 135M and 560M scales: SLORR-trained compressed models preserve performance substantially better than unregularized models while adding less than 1% average training overhead.

</details>


### [102] [Super Weights in LLMs and the Failure of Selective Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08733)
*Shreyas Subramanian,Adewale Akinfaderin,Akarsha Sehwag*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 超级权重（Super Weights）虽对模型性能至关重要，但单独训练这些参数会导致性能崩溃至随机猜测水平；参数重要性不等于可训练性，有效微调需依赖结构化分解而非针对单个重要权重。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期研究发现存在“超级权重”——移除这些参数会导致模型性能下降数个数量级。作者质疑：如果这些参数如此重要，那么针对超级权重的训练是否应该有效？这一研究旨在探索参数重要性与可训练性之间的关系。

Method: 在OLMo-1B和OLMo-7B模型上：1) 单独训练超级权重（100-8,192个参数）；2) 扩展到局部邻域（最多36K参数）；3) 对照实验：训练相同数量随机选择的位置；4) 测试Vanilla LoRA和应用于down_proj的低秩更新；5) 进行10-seed消融实验，约束超级权重位置的LoRA更新。

Result: 单独训练超级权重导致准确率降至随机猜测水平；扩展到局部邻域无改善；对照组（随机位置训练）优于基线；Vanilla LoRA（仅0.16%参数）和down_proj低秩更新均成功；约束超级权重位置的LoRA更新与对照组无统计差异。失败源于超级权重坐标本身，而非稀疏性。

Conclusion: 参数重要性并不意味着该参数在隔离状态下可训练；有效的微调方法依赖于对整个层的结构化分解（如LoRA的低秩结构），而非针对单个重要权重进行训练。这一发现对理解参数重要性理论与实际微调实践之间的关系具有重要意义。

Abstract: Recent work identified Super Weights, individual parameters whose removal degrades model performance by orders of magnitude. We show that this degradation due to pruning Super Weights does not universally apply to all LLMs. Furthermore, if these parameters are so important, Super Weight-aware training should be effective. We show the opposite. Training Super Weights in isolation (100 to 8,192 parameters) drops accuracy to random-guessing levels on both OLMo-1B and OLMo-7B, and expanding to local neighborhoods of up to 36K parameters provides no improvement. The failure is specific to Super Weight coordinates: training an equal number of randomly chosen positions in the same down_proj layers instead improves over the baseline, so the collapse comes from targeting Super Weights, not from sparsity itself. Vanilla LoRA, updating every position in attention weight matrices through low-rank structure, succeeds with only 0.16% of parameters, and applying the same low-rank update to down_proj succeeds as well. A 10-seed ablation confirms that constraining LoRA updates at positions corresponding to Super Weight coordinates yields statistically indistinguishable results. These findings establish that parameter importance does not imply parameter trainability in isolation, and that effective fine-tuning relies on structured decompositions over entire layers rather than targeting individually important weights.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [103] [AI-Driven Thermal Mapping and Management in 3D Integrated Photonic Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07711)
*Liton Kumar Biswas,Katayoon Yahyaei,Shajib Ghosh,M Shafkat M Khan,Himanandhan Reddy Kottur,Rayhane Ghane-Motlagh,Mahdi Nikdast,Navid Asadizanjani*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合AI驱动热建模和启发式设计方法的混合解决方案，用于解决三维光子集成电路的热管理挑战，实现了更准确和可扩展的实时热映射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三维光子集成电路(PICs)在高性能计算、数据中心和传感领域快速发展，但由于高密度键合和异质材料导致严重的热管理问题。传统方法如热显微镜和封装内传感器只能提供稀疏数据，无法获得完整的热分布可见性，限制了有效的热管理。

Method: 采用双重方法：1）AI驱动热建模框架，将稀疏传感器数据与设计层和密度信息集成，预测多层温度变化；2）基于设计的启发式方法，利用局部材料特性、设计布局、组件几何形状和传感器坐标细化特定区域的热估算，通过设计区域间的比较分析调整局部热阻率。启发式方法作为AI模型的补充，在无需大量训练数据的情况下提高估算精度。

Result: 成功生成了三维PIC的二维热图，通过插值传感器数据并结合局部热阻率调整实现。两种方法的结合提供了更高的估算精度，相比单一方法显著改善了热映射性能，且对训练数据的依赖性更低。

Conclusion: 该双重方法为下一代三维光子系统提供了可扩展、准确的热映射解决方案，支持实时热监测和设计阶段仿真，为实现可靠的热管理奠定了技术基础，具有重要的工程应用价值。

Abstract: Photonic Integrated Circuits (PICs) are advancing high-performance computing, data centers, and sensing, yet three-dimensional (3D) PICs introduce critical thermal management challenges due to high-density bonding and heterogeneous materials. Traditional methods like thermal microscopes and in-package sensors yield sparse data, limiting full thermal profile visibility. This paper presents a dual-method solution combining an AI-driven thermal modeling framework with a design-based heuristic approach. The AI method integrates sparse sensor data with design layer and density information to predict multilayer temperature variations, while the heuristic approach uses localized material properties, design layout, component geometries, and sensor coordinates to refine thermal estimations in specific regions. A 2D thermal map of a 3D PIC is generated by interpolating sensor data and adjusting for local thermal resistivity using comparative analysis between design regions. The heuristic method complements the AI model, improving estimation accuracy without extensive training data. Together, these methods offer a scalable, accurate solution for real-time thermal mapping and design-time simulation, enabling reliable thermal management in next-generation 3D photonic systems.

</details>


### [104] [A Theoretical Framework for Stochastic Activity Prediction in Tensor Accelerator Wallace-Tree Multipliers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08002)
*Prashanthi Metku,Chandra Gandu*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出随机活动预测(SAP)技术，通过检测操作数的汉明重量来预测乘法器的低开关活动，在确认正确时冻结输入，从而解决张量加速器乘法器在稀疏操作数下仍消耗动态功率的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 张量加速器乘法器即使在处理稀疏操作数时，每个时钟周期仍会消耗动态功率。现有技术无法解决此问题：零检测要求操作数恰好为零，结构性功率门控需要乘法器空闲，离线权重选择无法响应运行时数据。

Method: 提出随机活动预测(SAP)：在乘法器执行前检查到达操作数的汉明重量，预测低开关活动，当安全控制器独立确认重用正确时冻结输入。误预测仅导致节省机会错失，不会产生错误结果。基于三个形式化结论：谱收缩引理（Wallace树活动依赖操作数位密度，建立Lipschitz常数Lφ=3/2）、信息保留定理（每位每周期捕获O(n²)内部节点几乎所有预测信息）、伯努利最优性定理（所选拥码在汉明重量统计校准一位编码器中达到最优）。

Result: 对256周期窗口，预测误差低于10^-13；信息保留率η_I ≥ 1 - O(log n/n)，一位编码即可捕获O(n²)内部节点几乎所有预测信息；所选拥码在汉明重量统计的校准一位编码器族中被证明最优。

Conclusion: SAP填补了张量加速器功耗优化技术栈中现有技术未能覆盖的特定层次，为稀疏计算场景下的动态功耗优化提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Tensor accelerator multipliers burn dynamic power on every clock cycle, even when sparse operands require very little internal switching. No existing technique addresses this: zero-detection requires exactly-zero operands, structural power gating requires an idle multiplier, and offline weight selection cannot respond to runtime data. This paper introduces Stochastic Activity Prediction (SAP), which closes this gap by examining the Hamming weight of arriving operands before the multiplier executes, predicting low switching activity, and freezing the inputs when a deterministic Safety Controller independently confirms the reuse is correct. Mispredictions cause missed savings, never wrong answers. Three formal results underpin SAP: (i) a Spectral Contraction Lemma proving that Wallace-tree activity depends on operand bit density, not bit position, establishing Lipschitz constant $Lφ= 3/2$ and prediction error below $10^{-13}$ for a 256-cycle window; (ii) an Information Retention Theorem showing $η_I \ge 1 - O(\log n/n)$, so one bit per cycle captures nearly all predictive information about $O(n^2)$ internal nodes; and (iii) a Bernoulli Optimality Theorem proving the chosen encoding is shown to be optimal, within the family of calibrated one-bit encoders of Hamming-weight statistics considered. SAP addresses the specific layer of the tensor accelerator power stack that existing techniques do not cover.

</details>


### [105] [CRIMP: Compact & Reliable DNN Inference on In-Memory Processing via Crossbar-Aligned Compression and Non-ideality Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08015)
*Shuo Huai,Hao Kong,Xiangzhong Luo,Shiqing Li,Ravi Subramaniam,Christian Makaya,Qian Lin,Weichen Liu*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个综合学习框架，在单一训练过程中同时解决IMP加速器的量化、剪枝和非理想性问题，实现无硬件开销的高精度整数模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于忆阻器的存内处理(IMP)加速器面临三大障碍：1)浮点运算与忆阻器不兼容，现有量化方案仍需浮点处理器处理缩放因子；2)冗余DNN参数占用大量忆阻器，现有剪枝方法需要跨忆阻器数据对齐，引入显著的存储和计算开销；3)写入变化等非理想忆阻器行为降低模型精度，现有补偿方法开销巨大。

Method: 提出三项技术创新并集成到统一训练框架中：1)复用忆阻器中的位移单元近似乘法缩放因子，避免浮点处理器；2)采用核组剪枝和忆阻器剪枝消除数据对齐所需的硬件单元；3)通过运行时感知的非理想性适应，利用忆阻器特征在训练阶段缓解器件非理想性的影响。

Result: 量化方案精度损失可忽略不计；剪枝方法相比最先进方法实现更高稀疏度和精度。在CIFAR-10数据集上，生成的整数化、剪枝且可靠的VGG-16和ResNet-56模型精度下降分别仅为2.19%和1.26%，且无硬件开销。

Conclusion: 该综合学习框架成功在单一训练过程中解决了IMP加速器的量化、剪枝和非理想性三大问题，在无硬件开销的前提下实现了高精度的整数化模型部署，为高效DNN推理提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Crossbar-based In-Memory Processing (IMP) accelerators achieve high-speed, low-power computing for deep neural networks (DNNs), but face three obstacles. First, floating-point (FP) arithmetic is incompatible with crossbars, and existing quantization schemes still require FP processors for scaling factors, incurring hardware overhead. Second, redundant DNN parameters occupy too many crossbars, and current IMP-aware pruning methods require data aligning across crossbars, introducing significant memory and computing overhead. Third, non-ideal crossbar behaviors such as write variations degrade the accuracy of deployed models, and existing compensation methods add substantial overhead. In this paper, we address all three problems within a single training process. We reuse bit-shift units in crossbars to approximately multiply scaling factors, avoiding FP processors. We apply kernel-group pruning and crossbar pruning to remove the hardware units needed for data aligning. We adopt runtime-aware non-ideality adaptation to relieve the impact of device non-ideality from the training stage by exploiting crossbar features. Integrating these three optimizations into one comprehensive learning framework reduces training overhead and improves accuracy. Experiments show that our quantization incurs a negligible accuracy drop, and our pruning achieves higher sparsity and accuracy than state-of-the-art methods. Our framework produces integer-only, pruned, and reliable VGG-16 and ResNet-56 models for CIFAR-10 on IMP accelerators, with accuracy drops of only 2.19% and 1.26%, respectively, without hardware overhead.

</details>


### [106] [Who Needs DRAM? We Have Fiber](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08407)
*Hannah Atmer,Thiemo Voigt,Yuan Yao,Stefanos Kaxiras*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出光纤存储架构，利用光纤作为可重循环延迟线存储器存储大语言模型权重等不可变数据，在10,000个AI加速器案例中消除冗余权重存储，权重传输能耗相比HBM3e降低70%以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI对高性能存储的巨大需求导致DRAM可用性和合约价格压力上升，超大规模数据中心扩张进一步加剧了全球DRAM供应紧张，亟需新型存储架构缓解内存压力。

Method: 提出数据并行光广播延迟线存储架构，集成空分复用多芯光纤(MCFs)、无源光抽头放大接口、共封装光学(CPO)和区域全光再生技术，将光纤重新定位为超大规模数据中心中不可变数据的主动可重循环延迟线存储器。

Result: 案例研究评估表明，光纤存储可在10,000个AI加速器上消除冗余权重存储，相比传统HBM3e配置将权重传输能耗降低超过70%。

Conclusion: 光纤存储为超大规模数据中心提供了一种可行的新型存储范式，能够有效缓解DRAM压力，显著降低AI工作负载的存储能耗和冗余。

Abstract: The rising pressure on DRAM availability and contract pricing reflects generative AI's massive high-performance memory requirements. This pressure is heavily compounded by hyperscale data center expansion, which now consumes a significant portion of global DRAM output. In this work, we propose a new architecture: Fiber Memory, which reimagines the role of optical fiber in a hyperscale data center, deploying it as an active, recirculating delay-line memory for immutable data, such as large language model (LLM) weights. We present a data-parallel optical broadcast delay-line memory architecture that accounts for fiber's physical realities. By incorporating space-division multiplexed multi-core fibers (MCFs), passive optical tap-and-amplify interfaces, co-packaged optics (CPO), and regional all-optical regeneration, our case study evaluation demonstrates that Fiber Memory can eliminate redundant weight storage across 10,000 AI accelerators and reduce weight-delivery energy by over 70% compared to traditional HBM3e configurations.

</details>


### [107] [FPGN: Redefining Ultra-Fast Programmable Gate-based Neural Acceleration with Differentiable LUTs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08427)
*Jiawei Liang,Haotong Qin,Linfeng Du,Xingyu Liu,Shangkun Li,Hui Yu,Michele Magno,Xinyu Chen,Jiang Xu,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出FPGN框架，实现端到端的LUT原生神经网络在FPGA上的纳秒级低延迟推理，相比现有方法最高降低205倍延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有FPGA加速器以算术运算为中心，而LUT原生神经网络虽具理论潜力，但受限于算法层面优化，缺乏与FPGA物理原语的匹配、布线与时序收敛问题，以及自动化设计空间探索流程，难以转化为高性能硬件实现。

Method: 提出FPGN端到端物理感知框架，包含三方面：(i) 硬件对齐的可微公式用于训练FPGA原生LUT神经元；(ii) 结构化LUT原生拓扑与流式硬件架构以提升布线局部性和时序收敛；(iii) 延迟驱动编译器，利用高保真分析性QoR模型自动化设计空间探索和硬件生成。

Result: 实验表明，与代表性FPGA BNN加速器相比，FPGN实现高达205倍延迟降低；相比先前可微LUT原生网络，LUT效率提升高达30倍，同时保持竞争性推理精度。

Conclusion: FPGN成功弥合LUT原生学习与延迟优化FPGA实现之间的差距，为深度神经网络在FPGA上实现纳秒级推理提供有效解决方案。

Abstract: Achieving nanosecond-scale inference latency for deep neural networks (DNNs) has become a primary architectural concern for latency-critical applications. While Field-Programmable Gate Arrays (FPGAs) offer a promising substrate for low-latency inference, conventional FPGA accelerators remain arithmetic-centric, using LUTs primarily as building blocks for numerical operators and peripheral logic. In contrast, recent LUT-native neural networks treat LUTs as learnable neurons, revealing promising theoretical potential to exploit their intrinsic logic expressivity. However, existing methods are largely confined to algorithmic optimizations, failing to translate this theoretical potential into high-performance FPGA accelerators. Specifically, their differentiable formulations do not faithfully match FPGA LUT primitives, their physically-unaware topologies compromise routability and timing closure, and their lack of automated optimization flow hinders systematic design space exploration (DSE) and efficient hardware implementation.
  In this paper, we propose FPGN, an end-to-end physically-aware framework that closes the gap between LUT-native learning and latency-optimized FPGA implementation. FPGN addresses these challenges through (i) a hardware-aligned differentiable formulation for training FPGA-native LUT neurons, (ii) a structured LUT-native topology with a streaming hardware architecture to improve routing locality and timing closure, and (iii) a latency-driven compiler that leverages high-fidelity analytical Quality of Results models to automate DSE and hardware generation. Experiments show that FPGN achieves up to 205$\times$ latency reduction compared to representative FPGA-based BNN accelerators and up to 30$\times$ higher LUT efficiency than prior differentiable LUT-native networks, while maintaining competitive inference accuracy.

</details>


<div id='cs.NI'></div>

# cs.NI [[Back]](#toc)

### [108] [MORES: Mobile Reasoning-as-a-Service via Distributed LLM Inference-Time Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08116)
*Guanchen Liu,Hongyang Du,Kaibin Huang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出MORES移动推理服务框架，通过边缘设备与服务器协作执行隐式推理，利用语义MoE深度强化学习算法优化计算与通信调度，实现系统吞吐量约18%的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理时扩展（包括显式推理和隐式推理）能增强大语言模型推理能力，但两者均产生巨大的计算和内存开销，难以部署在资源受限的边缘设备上。

Method: 提出移动推理即服务（MORES）框架，利用隐式推理的递归结构在边缘设备和服务器之间划分隐藏状态更新，实现协作推理；将问题建模为联合计算与通信调度问题，采用语义混合专家（MoE）深度强化学习算法求解，通过语义路由器实现高速门控以实时选择专家，自适应分配资源。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提方法相比基线Soft Actor-Critic（SAC）算法在系统吞吐量上实现约18%的提升。

Conclusion: MORES框架有效解决了边缘设备上推理时扩展的计算资源瓶颈问题，通过协作推理与智能调度实现了性能提升，为资源受限设备上的LLM推理提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Inference-time scaling has emerged as an effective approach for enhancing the capabilities of Large Language Models (LLMs), addressing the growing demand for stronger reasoning without increasing model size. This novel form of LLM scaling comprises two representative approaches: explicit reasoning, which generates intermediate chain-of-thought tokens during an explicit thinking phase, and implicit reasoning, which iteratively updates hidden states in the latent space without producing explicit outputs. Despite their effectiveness, both paradigms incur substantial computational and memory overhead, raising challenges for deployment on resource-constrained edge devices. To address these issues, we propose a Mobile Reasoning-as-aService (MORES) framework that treats reasoning as a computational service accessible to edge devices over wireless networks. Focusing on implicit reasoning, we leverage its recursive structure to partition hiddenstate updates between edge devices and servers, enabling cooperative inference that allows devices to access additional cloud computation on demand. To optimize long-term performance, we formulate a joint computation and communication scheduling problem and solve it using a semantic Mixture-of-Experts (MoE)-based Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL) algorithm to address heterogeneity in wireless conditions and task demands. The agent adaptively allocates resources by adjusting the number of recurrent steps and the transmission pruning rate, while a semantic router enables high-speed gating for real-time expert selection. Experimental results show that the proposed method achieves an approximately 18% improvement in system throughput over the baseline Soft Actor-Critic (SAC) algorithm. Our code is available at https://github.com/NICE-HKU/MORES.

</details>


### [109] [ADORN: Adaptive Drift handling for Open RAN using Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08443)
*Ashit Kumar Subudhi,Bhargav Chirumamilla,Shubham Vaishnav,Mduduzi C. Hlophe,Praveen Kumar Donta,Andrea Fumagalli,Venkateswarlu Gudepu,Koteswararao Kondepu*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Q学习的自适应重训练方法，通过将重训练决策建模为马尔可夫决策过程（MDP），在维持预测精度的同时显著降低了计算开销，有效应对了O-RAN中动态流量变化导致的模型漂移问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在开放式无线接入网（O-RAN）中，动态流量变化会导致人工智能/机器学习（AI/ML）模型发生漂移，从而降低模型性能。传统的重训练方法虽然能够维持预测精度，但存在计算成本高昂的问题，且可能导致违反服务等级协议（SLA）。因此，需要在保证预测精度的同时降低重训练开销。

Method: 提出了一种基于Q学习的自适应重训练方法，将重训练决策问题建模为马尔可夫决策过程（MDP）。强化学习（RL）代理学习一种策略，用于平衡预测精度和重训练成本。此外，该方法引入了多专家长短期记忆（LSTM）集成机制，以缓解灾难性遗忘问题，并提高模型在不同流量条件下的鲁棒性。

Result: 实验结果表明，与贪婪基线和随机基线相比，该方法能够有效减少重训练开销，同时将系统性能维持在预定义的限制范围内。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于强化学习的自适应重训练框架能够有效解决O-RAN中的模型漂移问题，在保持系统性能的同时降低了计算成本，为动态环境下的模型维护提供了一种高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Dynamic traffic variations in Open Radio Access Networks (O-RAN) lead to drift, which degrades the performance of Artificial Intelligence/Machine Learning (AI/ML) models. Traditional retraining approaches maintain forecasting accuracy but incur high computational cost and may lead to violations of Service Level Agreements (SLAs). This work proposes a Q-learning-based adaptive retraining approach that formulates the retraining decision as a Markov Decision Process (MDP), where a Reinforcement Learning (RL) agent learns a policy that balances forecasting accuracy and retraining cost. The proposed approach incorporates a multi-expert Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) ensemble to mitigate catastrophic forgetting and improve robustness across diverse traffic conditions. Experimental results show that the proposed approach effectively reduces retraining overhead compared to greedy and random baselines, while maintaining system performance within predefined limits.

</details>


### [110] [Spatio-Temporal Scheduling Prediction Under Backhaul Delay for Resilient Coordinated Beamforming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08454)
*Prashant Kumar Singh,Shubham Vaishnav,Ahmet Hasim Gökceoglu,Li Wang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 针对5G分布式网络中回程延迟导致协调波束成形性能下降的问题，提出基于谱时序图神经网络(StemGNN)的两阶段预测框架，通过预测未来用户调度状态来替代过时信息，在1 TTI延迟下恢复了57-73%的和速率损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在分布式5G网络中，协调波束成形(CBF)依赖于基站间调度信息的及时交换，但回程延迟会导致信息过时。即使仅1个传输时间间隔(TTI)的延迟，也会使CBF-SLNR性能降至非协调基线以下，因为预编码器会向已不再活跃的用户抑制干扰。在过时信息上进行协调反而比不协调更糟糕。

Method: 提出两阶段预测框架：第一阶段采用谱时序图神经网络(StemGNN)从延迟的历史观测中预测未来用户设备(UE)调度状态；第二阶段将预测结果替代过时输入，输入到CBF-SLNR预编码器中。在三小区大规模MIMO下行链路场景下进行验证，配置60个UE，每基站64天线，采用Quadriga城市微小区(UMi)信道模型和比例公平调度器。

Result: StemGNN实现87.57%的平均调度预测准确率，在所有评估时间范围内均优于LSTM、GRU、简单RNN和马尔可夫链基线，在更长时间范围内相比LSTM提升高达7.71%。当集成到协调波束成形中时，预测恢复了57-73%因1 TTI回程延迟造成的和速率损失，相比无预测基线和速率提升9.58-14.35%，恢复高达83%的小区边缘用户Lag-1公平性损失。

Conclusion: 将回程延迟视为时空预测问题是在延迟受限网络中实现稳健小区间协调的有效方法，该方法不仅能恢复大部分吞吐量损失，还能在更长时间范围内保持公平性增益。

Abstract: Coordinated beamforming in distributed 5G networks relies on the timely exchange of inter-cell scheduling information, but backhaul latency makes this information stale. Even a single transmission time interval (TTI) of delay can reduce CBF-SLNR performance below the uncoordinated baseline, because the precoder suppresses interference toward users that are no longer active. Coordination on stale information is therefore worse than no coordination at all. To address this, we propose a two-stage predictive framework in which a Spectral Temporal Graph Neural Network (StemGNN) predicts future user equipment (UE) scheduling states from delayed historical observations, and the predictions replace stale inputs to the CBF-SLNR precoder. Evaluated on a three-cell massive MIMO downlink with 60 UEs and 64 antennas per base station under Quadriga Urban Micro (UMi) channels and a proportional fair scheduler, StemGNN achieves a mean scheduling prediction accuracy of 87.57%, outperforming LSTM, GRU, Simple RNN, and Markov chain baselines at all evaluated horizons, with gains of up to 7.71% over LSTM at longer horizons where inter-UE structural dependencies dominate over temporal autocorrelation. When integrated into coordinated beamforming, the predictions recover 57-73% of the sum rate loss caused by one TTI of backhaul delay, improving sum rate by 9.58-14.35% over the no-prediction baseline and recovering up to 83% of the Lag-1 fairness loss for cell-edge users, with fairness gains persisting at higher lag values where throughput gains diminish. These results show that treating backhaul latency as a spatio-temporal forecasting problem is an effective approach for robust inter-cell coordination in delay-constrained networks.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [111] [ASMR: Agentic Schema Generation for Ship Maintenance Report Writing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08177)
*Sohrab Namazi Nia,Amogh Dalal,Ning Sa,Peter Ly,Marti Zentmaier,Tomek Strzalkowski,Jay Miller,Rishi Singh,Senjuti Basu Roy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ASMR框架，通过两个专门Agent（字段生成Agent和结构优化Agent）自动从历史船舶维护和运营报告中生成紧凑且信息丰富的模式，以指导报告作者产出更完整、一致和可操作的报告。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 船舶维护和运营报告存在多种表格类别，历史报告包含丰富的叙述性数据。手动设计模式既耗时又难以捕捉所有必要的信息需求，因此需要自动化方法从历史数据中发现紧凑且信息丰富的模式，以改善未来报告的质量和一致性。

Method: 提出ASMR模块化智能体框架，包含两个专门Agent：1）字段生成Agent：从历史叙述中提取语义概念，通过自适应多粒度聚类生成候选模式字段；2）结构优化Agent：采用强化学习识别紧凑、信息丰富且非冗余的模式表示。

Result: 初步结果验证了所提方法的可行性，能够自动生成紧凑且信息丰富的模式，但同时也揭示了在数据管理、智能体AI和以人为中心AI交叉领域的若干开放研究挑战。

Conclusion: ASMR框架为自动模式生成问题提供了有效解决方案，生成的模式可指导报告作者产出更完整、一致和可操作的报告，该工作在数据管理与智能体AI结合领域具有重要研究价值。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the automatic schema generation problem: given a collection of historical ship maintenance and operational reports across multiple form categories, automatically discover compact and informative schemas that capture the essential information requirements of each report type. To address this challenge, we propose ASMR, a modular agentic framework consisting of two specialized agents. A Field Generation Agent extracts semantic concepts from historical narratives and generates candidate schema fields through adaptive multi-granularity clustering, while a Structural Optimizer Agent employs reinforcement learning to identify compact, informative, and non-redundant schema representations. The resulting schemas can guide report authors toward producing more complete, consistent, and actionable reports. Preliminary results demonstrate the promise of the proposed approach and highlight several open research challenges at the intersection of data management, agentic AI, and human-centered AI.

</details>


### [112] [Context Graphs for Proactive Enterprise Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07721)
*Avinash Kumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于上下文图谱的前瞻式智能体框架，通过Delta检测引擎和前瞻性评分器实现主动推送，在三个企业案例中精度达0.83，平均响应时间从47分钟降至30秒内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG和智能体框架本质上是被动的，需等待人类查询才能行动。企业真正的生产力提升需要前瞻式智能体：在员工提问前主动推送相关、可操作的信息。

Method: 构建上下文图谱——一种动态关系数据结构，建模企业实体、关系和状态变迁；在此基础上设计Delta检测引擎持续监控状态变化，前瞻性评分器根据紧迫性、相关性和角色适配度对候选洞察排序，以及LLM驱动的推送层提供带解释的排序通知。文中形式化了各组件，推导了统一的前瞻性评分函数，并提供了基于NetworkX和Anthropic Claude API的完整Python实现。

Result: 在合同生命周期管理、工程事件响应和销售管道健康度三个通用企业案例研究中，基于上下文图谱的前瞻机制实现了Precision@5达0.83，假阳性率0.11，平均推送时间从被动基线的47分钟降至30秒以内。

Conclusion: 上下文图谱驱动的企业前瞻式智能体具有可行性和实用性，可显著提升生产力和响应速度。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) and agentic frameworks have advanced enterprise AI considerably, yet agents remain fundamentally reactive: they wait for a human query before acting. This paper argues that genuine enterprise productivity gains require proactive agents: systems that surface relevant, actionable information to workers before they ask. We propose the Context Graph, a live relational data structure that models enterprise entities, their relationships, and state transitions over time. Built on this graph, we define a Delta Detection Engine that continuously monitors state changes, a Proactivity Scorer that ranks candidate insights by urgency, relevance, and persona-fit, and a Surfacing Layer powered by an LLM that delivers ranked notifications with grounded explanations. We formalize each component, derive a unified Proactivity Score function, and provide a complete end-to-end Python implementation using NetworkX and the Anthropic Claude API. Evaluation across three generic enterprise case studies (contract lifecycle management, engineering incident response, and sales pipeline hygiene) demonstrates that context-graph-driven proactivity achieves Precision@5 of 0.83, a false positive rate of 0.11, and reduces mean time to surface from 47 minutes (reactive baseline) to under 30 second.

</details>


### [113] [AI-integrated models for assessing agricultural resilience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07759)
*Joshua R. Waite,Dana Golden,Brett Indelicato,Kevin Camp,Mojdeh Saadati,Shannon Regan,Patrick Schnable,Baskar Ganapathysubramanian,Carlos Messina,Suzanne Thornsbury,Soumik Sarkar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究开发了一款AI工具，通过整合经济模型（GTAP）和生物物理模型（APSIM），利用自然语言交互分析农业供应链中断的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 农业供应链容易受到相互关联的生物物理和经济系统中断的影响，缺乏跨学科的分析工具来评估这些冲击。

Method: 开发了一个AI驱动的工具，将经济模型（GTAP）与生物物理模型（APSIM）相结合，支持用户通过自然语言进行查询并获得响应。

Result: 成功构建了一个能够分析供应链冲击的AI工具，使政策制定者和市场参与者能够评估跨学科的影响。

Conclusion: 该工具为评估农业供应链中断提供了跨学科的解决方案，增强了决策者应对供应链风险的能力。

Abstract: Agricultural supply chains are vulnerable to disruptions through linked biophysical and economic systems. We develop an AI-powered tool that integrates economic models (GTAP) with biophysical models (APSIM) to analyze supply chain shocks, enabling policymakers and market participants to assess cross-disciplinary impacts through queries and responses written in natural language.

</details>


### [114] [Adversarial Social Epistemology for Assemblies of Humans and Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07760)
*Mihnea C. Moldoveanu,Joel A. C. Baum*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了对抗性社会认识论（ASE）框架，用于分析密集交互的传播环境中，主体如何利用和破坏支撑公共断言的信任机制以谋取私利。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的认识泡沫、回声室和错误信息传播等概念无法充分解释密集交互传播环境中的信任操纵现象。在这些环境中，传播主体有动机和能力为了私人、声誉、修辞或物质利益而扭曲、着色、遗漏、伪造或策略性地模糊信息，其核心问题在于主体如何利用和破坏支撑性断言的可信任性。

Method: 作者开发了一套理论语言和分析框架，基于认识网络，结合推论主义语义学来解释断言。该框架关注主体如何利用支撑性断言中承诺和权利的正常信任机制，并识别出破坏信任的可审计性机制。

Result: 研究提供了一个分析支撑性公共传播中信任破坏机制的理论框架，揭示了主体如何利用推论链条的可审计性来破坏信任，并为审计和纠正信任违背提供了机制性工具。

Conclusion: 对抗性社会认识论框架能够有效分析和解释密集交互传播环境中信任被操纵和破坏的现象，为理解和应对支撑性公共传播中的信任危机提供了新的理论工具和实践指导。

Abstract: We outline an adversarial social epistemology (ASE) for densely interactive communicative landscapes in which public assertions are scaffolded by chains of testimony, inference, institutional certification, and tacit trust. In such landscapes, agents have incentives and affordances to distort, color, omit, fabricate, or strategically under-specify information for private, reputational, rhetorical, or material gains. We argue that these phenomena are not adequately captured by familiar descriptions of epistemic bubbles, echo chambers, or misinformation diffusion. What requires explanation is how communicative agents exploit the commitments and entitlements that normally make scaffolded assertions trustworthy. We provide language that delivers the requisite analysis, outline mechanisms that subvert trust in scaffolded public communications, and outline machinery for auditing and redressing trust breaches arising from subverting the auditability of inferential chains, drawing on epistemic networks, enriched with an inferentialist semantics for interpreting assertions.

</details>


### [115] [Aligning Clinical Needs and AI Capabilities: A Survey on LLMs for Medical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07761)
*Qi Peng,Jiatong Li,Sirui Huang,Yiyang Jiang,Kaisong Gong,Ronger Ding,Shijie Ye,Changmeng Zheng,Yi Cai,Xiaobo Yang,Jin Huang,Xiao-Yong Wei,Qing Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文综述了医学大语言模型的最新进展，提出了基于Miller金字塔的五级临床能力框架，构建了涵盖五个推理能力层级的基准数据集，测试了18个模型，发现专科模型在诊断任务上占优而通用模型在决策支持和对话方面领先。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型在医疗健康领域的重要性日益凸显，亟需系统地理解其在临床推理和患者护理中的应用现状与要求，以促进临床实践与计算方法的有机结合。

Method: 采用双重视角方法：临床层面基于Miller金字塔建立五级能力体系（从知识回忆到动态病例管理）；计算层面将演绎、归纳和外展推理模式与医疗目标和任务相联系；构建覆盖五个医学推理能力层级的基准数据集，对18个前沿模型进行评估。

Result: 构建了医学推理能力基准数据集，评估结果显示：医学专科模型在以诊断为中心的任务中表现优异，而通用模型在决策支持和对话任务中更具优势；揭示了当前模型在不同推理层级上的能力差异。

Conclusion: 总结了当前进展与开放性挑战，包括数据局限性、幻觉问题和知识落地问题，并提出了迈向更安全、更可靠、更适应临床工作流程系统的发展方向。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have emerged as important tools in healthcare, showing growing potential for clinical reasoning and patient care. This survey examines recent progress in medical LLMs, focusing on reasoning applications and requirements. We present a dual-view approach that connects clinical practice with computational methods. On the clinical side, we establish a five-level competency scheme following Miller's Pyramid, progressing from knowledge recall to dynamic case management. On the computational side, we link deductive, inductive, and abductive reasoning patterns to common medical goals and tasks. We also introduce a benchmark dataset spanning five levels of medical reasoning capability and report results on 18 state-of-the-art models, revealing that medical specialist models excel in diagnosis-centric tasks while general models lead in decision support and dialogue. We conclude by discussing current progress and open challenges, including data limitations, hallucination, and grounding issues, and outline directions toward safer, more reliable, and workflow-ready systems.

</details>


### [116] [Alignment Plausibility: A New Standard for Assuring AI in Healthcare](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07766)
*Gwydion Williams,Sara Zannone,Bilal A Mateen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出“alignment plausibility”（对齐合理性）监管框架，借鉴临床实践的三层保障（价值规范、训练嵌入、监督检测），以应对大语言模型在心理健康支持中的长期风险与商业激励冲突，为AI医疗系统的安全性与可信度提供评估标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在心理健康支持领域应用广泛，但其商业逻辑偏向于维持用户参与，而非提供有效的心理支持，导致开发者的安全措施往往被动应对显性伤害，忽视依赖性、边界模糊、扭曲信念放大等长期风险。缺乏结构化的安全保障机制，亟需借鉴临床实践的安全保障模式，构建系统性的对齐框架。

Method: 提出对齐合理性框架，参照临床实践的三层保障机制：（1）明确的价值规范——基于临床实践的规范化承诺；（2）训练嵌入——将价值规范嵌入模型训练过程；（3）监督机制——在部署过程中检测偏移与长期伤害，类似于临床督导。通过类比生物学合理性（biological plausibility）这一成熟监管概念，论证该框架的可行性。

Result: 成功构建了“对齐合理性”这一监管概念，为AI健康系统提供了论证系统安全性、无害性与患者获益的原则性框架，填补了现有监管工具在评估AI系统长期安全性方面的空白。

Conclusion: 对齐合理性为AI在医疗健康领域的监管提供了新思路，可作为一种评估系统是否真正对齐于正向健康结果、不会造成伤害、最终惠及患者的结构性论证工具，具有实践指导意义。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have become significant providers of mental health support, yet they remain products of an attention economy whose operational and commercial targets favour sustained engagement over the friction that effective psychological support often requires. Developers' safety responses have been largely reactive, addressing the most visible and acute harms while subtler, longer-term patterns of risk (e.g., dependency, boundary erosion, the amplification of distorted beliefs) receive less attention. We contend that making LLMs structurally safe requires alignment organised at three levels that mirror how society assures the safety of human clinical practice: 1) explicit value specification grounded in the codified normative commitments of clinical practice; 2) training that embeds those values in the model; and 3) oversight that detects drift and longer-term harm during deployment, much as clinical supervision does for human practice. Organising alignment in this way yields a construct we call alignment plausibility - a structured demonstration that a system's values, training regime, and oversight mechanisms are together consistent with safe and positive outcomes. We propose alignment plausibility as a regulatory construct (by drawing analogy to the established construct of biological plausibility) for AI in health: a principled way to argue for, or against, trust that systems are aligned to positive health outcomes, will cause no harm even where capable of doing so, and will ultimately lead to patient benefit.

</details>


### [117] [Idiobionics: The Unification of Privacy and Intelligent Robotic Prostheses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07775)
*Kwesi Afari Darfoor,Patrick M. Pilarski,Bailey Kacsmar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了"个体生物电子学"(idiobionics)这一新研究方向，旨在研究智能仿生肢体等紧密耦合生物-数字系统中的隐私风险与对抗性攻击问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着机器人假肢(仿生肢体)集成了先进的传感器和人工智能控制系统，这些设备已成为半自主的可穿戴机器人系统。然而，同样的感知与控制进步也引入了可被恶意实体利用的威胁载体，可能侵犯用户隐私。这些隐私风险可能阻碍用户接受下一代仿生肢体技术。

Method: 本文提出了"idiobionics"这一新研究脉络，将其植根于相关文献基础之上，并提供了初步证据，展示和讨论了可能利用智能仿生肢体设计的潜在对抗性攻击。同时整理了一份与可穿戴机器人及其他面向人类的自主系统研究者相关的开放性研究问题清单。

Result: 定义了idiobionics概念，并通过初步证据揭示了智能仿生肢体设计中存在的潜在对抗性攻击，为该领域建立了系统性的研究框架和开放性问题清单。

Conclusion: idiobionics研究将有助于充分释放机器人假肢及相关仿生设备的潜力，在推动技术进步的同时保障用户隐私安全，促进用户采纳。

Abstract: The human body is at the center of a growing family of technologies designed to tightly and persistently couple biological and digital systems. Robotic prostheses are a representative example of this tight coupling. Also referred to as bionic limbs, robotic prostheses are devices that support people who have lost limbs in pursuing daily life activities such as walking and grasping objects. Bionic limbs are now perceptive and responsive owing to their integration with advanced sensors and artificial intelligence-based control approaches. Consequently, such robotic prostheses can now be viewed as semiautonomous wearable robotic systems that can co-adapt with their users. However, the same sensing and control advancements that increase the capability of robotic prostheses also introduce threat vectors that could be exploited by malicious entities to violate the privacy of users. To fully realize the benefits of next-generation bionic limbs, we maintain it is important to directly understand and address these privacy risks and the barriers they might present to user adoption. This paper therefore introduces a new line of inquiry we term idiobionics to holistically investigate issues at the intersection of privacy and intelligent bionic limbs. As the main contribution of this paper, we define idiobionics, ground it in related literature, and provide preliminary evidence showing and discussing potential adversarial attacks that could exploit intelligent bionic limb designs. We then contribute a curated list of open research questions within idiobionics that are relevant to researchers in wearable robotics and other human-facing autonomous systems. We expect that idiobionics research will help unlock the full potential of robotic prostheses and related bionic devices.

</details>


### [118] [Infinity-Parser2 Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07836)
*Zuming Huang,Jun Huang,Kexuan Ren,Baode Wang,Weizhen Li,Jianming Feng,Yu Wang,Yichen Yao,Shijun Lin,Yige Tang,Cheng Peng,Weidi Xu,Wei Chu,Yinghui Xu,Yuan Qi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Infinity-Parser2，一个大规模多模态文档解析模型，通过可控数据合成管线与多任务强化学习，解决了标注语料库稀缺问题，在多个基准测试中达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文档解析领域长期面临高质量标注语料库稀缺的挑战，现有方法难以获得忠实标注的解析数据集，限制了端到端文档解析模型的发展。

Method: 1) 构建可扩展的数据合成引擎，结合可控渲染框架与迭代优化循环，创建并开源500万样本的中英双语语料库Infinity-Doc2-5M，包含元素边界框、规范内容格式及阅读顺序标注；2) 设计可验证的多任务奖励系统，通过联合强化学习同时优化8个任务目标（文档解析、布局分析、表格/公式/图表/化学式解析、文档VQA及多模态理解），统一感知、结构与推理。

Result: 发布两个模型变体：Infinity-Parser2-Flash（相比前代吞吐量提升3.68倍）和Infinity-Parser2-Pro（精度优化版本）。Infinity-Parser2-Pro在olmOCR-Bench达到87.6%，ParseBench达到74.3%，超越DeepSeek-OCR-2、PaddleOCR-VL-1.5和MinerU2.5，在图表、化学公式和文档VQA任务上展现强泛化能力。

Conclusion: Infinity-Parser2通过大规模合成数据与多任务强化学习的结合，有效解决了文档解析语料稀缺问题，实现了统一的感知-结构-推理优化，推动了端到端文档解析技术的发展。

Abstract: We present Infinity-Parser2, a large multimodal model that couples a controllable data-synthesis pipeline with multi-task reinforcement learning for end-to-end document parsing, addressing the persistent scarcity of faithfully annotated parsing corpora. Our contributions are threefold. First, we build a scalable synthesis engine, pairing a controllable rendering framework with an iterative refinement loop, and use it to construct and open-source Infinity-Doc2-5M: a 5-million-sample bilingual (Chinese/English) corpus spanning diverse document types, annotated with element bounding boxes, canonical content forms (Markdown, HTML, LaTeX, SMILES, structured charts), and full-page reading order. Second, we introduce a verifiable, multi-task reward system that enables Joint Reinforcement Learning across eight co-trained objectives (document parsing, layout analysis, table parsing, math formula parsing, chart parsing, chemical formula parsing, document VQA, and general multimodal understanding), unifying perception, structure, and reasoning in a single optimization signal. Third, we release two variants under a shared architecture: Infinity-Parser2-Flash, optimized for low-latency inference with a $3.68\times$ throughput gain over Infinity-Parser-7B, and Infinity-Parser2-Pro, engineered for precision-critical settings. Infinity-Parser2-Pro reaches state-of-the-art 87.6% on olmOCR-Bench and 74.3% on ParseBench, surpassing DeepSeek-OCR-2, PaddleOCR-VL-1.5, and MinerU2.5, with strong generalization to charts, chemical formulas, and document VQA.

</details>


### [119] [VectorizationLLM: Smart Vectorization Based AI Assistant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07846)
*Ryan Duke*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个基于Google开源LLM构建的专用大语言模型VectorizationLLM，旨在辅助学生学习MATLAB中的向量化、时间/波向量分析、分段函数、傅里叶分析和微分方程，采用RAG技术和系统提示架构设计，通过提供概念解释而非直接答案的方式辅助教学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了帮助纽约理工学院电气与计算机工程技术系CTEC 247课程《应用计算分析II》的学生更好地学习MATLAB中的向量化及相关数学概念，需要一个能够提供详细解释而非直接答案的教学辅助工具。

Method: 基于Google开源大语言模型构建VectorizationLLM，采用RAG（检索增强生成）知识库和系统提示架构，整合课堂笔记中的示例，设计为教学助手而非直接答案生成器，能够提供包含代码、文本和图像的详细解释。

Result: 成功构建了一个专门用于MATLAB向量化教学的大语言模型，能够为学生提供向量化、时间/波向量分析、分段函数、傅里叶分析和微分方程等概念的详细解释和示例，而不会直接给出问题答案。

Conclusion: VectorizationLLM展示了专用大语言模型在工程教育中的应用潜力，通过RAG技术和适当的教学设计，可以创建有效的教学辅助工具，帮助学生在不依赖直接答案的情况下深入理解复杂的数学和编程概念。

Abstract: VectorizationLLM is a specialized Large Language Model based on Google open-weight LLMs. The model is designed to assist students to learn smart vectorization, time/wave vector analysis, piecewise functions, Fourier analysis, and differential equations in MATLAB. The course application is CTEC 247: Applied Computational Analysis II by the Department of Electrical & Computer Engineering Technology at New York Institute of Technology Old Westbury. The LLM model is designed to be an instructive assistant, providing detailed explanations of concepts with examples from in-class notes without providing direct answers to questions. The model is designed with a RAG (Retrieval Augmented Generation) knowledge base and system prompt architecture. Examples in both code, text, and images are provided in the LLM responses.

</details>


### [120] [A Graph Neural Network Model for Real-Time Gesture Recognition Based on sEMG Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07850)
*Pragatheeswaran Vipulanandan,Kamal Premaratne,Manohar Murthi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于图神经网络的sEMG手势识别新方法，在8名受试者测试中达到99%的分类准确率，单次预测仅需48ms，适用于实时应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了实现对先进假肢和增强现实的流畅控制，需要准确且即时的手势识别。前臂表面肌电信号(sEMG)是常用的手势识别手段，但需要更高效的方法来提升识别性能。

Method: 开发了一种新颖的sEMG表示方法，利用图网络来编码前臂肌肉激活模式信息。基于这些图网络，构建了一个图神经网络机器学习算法用于实时手势识别。实验使用具有8个电极的myoband采集前臂sEMG信号，涉及8名健康受试者。

Result: 所提方法的平均分类准确率达到99%，超越了现有最先进技术的性能。在M1 pro CPU上，图构建和预测的平均时间合计为48ms，满足实时性要求。

Conclusion: 该方法在保持高识别准确率的同时实现了实时性能，证明了图神经网络在sEMG手势识别任务中的有效性，为假肢控制和增强现实应用提供了可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: For seemless control of advanced hand prostheses and augmented reality, accurate and immediate hand gestures recognition is essential. Surface electromyography (sEMG) signals obtained from the forearm are commonly employed for this purpose. In this paper, we present a novel approach for sEMG representation that utilizes graph networks which contain information about muscle activation patterns in the forearm. Based on these graph networks, we have developed a machine learning algorithm capable of real-time hand gesture recognition using a graph neural network. The algorithm's performance was evaluated using sEMG signals acquired from myoband, which has 8 electrodes placed around the forearm, involving 8 healthy subjects. The proposed method demonstrated an average classification accuracy of 99\%, surpassing the performance of state-of-the-art techniques. The average time for both graph construction and prediction stood at 48ms utilizing a M1 pro CPU, rendering the approach well-suited for real-time applications.

</details>


### [121] [Agentic AI and Retrieval-Augmented Models in Straight-Through Underwriting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07858)
*Robert Richardson,Josh Meyers,Brian Hartman,David Sandberg*

Main category: cs.AI
